summary = "OPSRD以专家角色提示作为特权教学上下文，通过同一基础模型的在策略自蒸馏，在无需参考答案且推理时不使用角色提示的情况下提升数学推理能力。"
paper = "https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.39884"
code = "https://github.com/zhansan114514/OPSRD"
dataset = ""
link = ""
content = "OPSRD让无角色提示的学生模型生成在策略轨迹，再由冻结的同源模型基于相同前缀输出角色条件化的词元分布。该方法不依赖参考答案，即使完整采样结果错误，也能利用教师在下一词元层面提供的有效偏好。训练采用教师加权的前向 KL 散度，并通过裁剪限制单个词表项的影响，同时仅监督学生预测熵最高的一半位置。Qwen3-1.7B、4B 和 8B 在三个竞赛数学基准上的实验表明，模型在推理阶段移除角色提示后仍优于基础模型，且前向 KL 在各规模下均取得最高宏平均准确率。"
purpose = "- 展示了如何把角色提示产生的专家行为内化到模型参数中，从而消除推理阶段对角色提示的依赖。\n- 说明完整答案错误并不意味着监督信号无效，逐词元分布仍可提供有价值的替代选择。\n- 教师加权前向 KL 可覆盖学生低估、较少采样的候选词元，为在策略蒸馏目标设计提供参考。\n- 基于预测熵筛选监督位置，有助于将训练计算集中在模型最不确定、最具学习价值的决策点。"
notes = "标题：OPSRD: On-Policy Self-Role Distillation\n来源：arXiv\n链接：https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.39884\n摘要：Role prompting elicits specialized behavior from large language models through an expert identity, offering a lightweight way to guide reasoning on demanding tasks. However, evaluating or distilling complete role-prompted answers can miss useful next-token preferences when the sampled solution remains incorrect. Transferring these preferences also requires an objective that reaches alternatives the student rarely predicts. We introduce OPSRD, which uses a fixed expert role as privileged teaching context for on-policy self-distillation without reference solutions. A role-free student generates a trajectory, and a frozen instance of the same base model supplies role-conditioned distributions on its exact prefixes, exposing alternatives beyond the sampled continuation. Teacher-weighted forwar"

content = "SEAR 是一个包含四类任务的音频问答基准，覆盖声学证据识别与量化、音频深伪检测以及取证解释生成。研究发现，六种音频语言模型生成的合理解释与其可验证的声学证据推理能力之间存在明显差距。作者进一步提出 BAEA 智能体，为冻结的音频语言模型配备受控声学工具，并支持固定或自适应的证据获取策略。实验显示 BAEA-Fixed 能在两个评测分区上改善最终判定和取证解释，而误导性证据会同时削弱检测与证据落地表现。"
purpose = "- 为音频深伪检测提供兼顾最终结论、证据定位与解释可信度的细粒度评测框架。\n- 揭示“解释看似合理”并不等同于模型真正利用了可验证的声学证据。\n- 说明外接受控声学工具可在不更新模型参数的情况下增强音频取证能力。\n- 可用于研究证据干预、工具调用策略及音频语言模型的可靠性与抗误导能力。"
notes = "标题：SEAR: Spoofing Evidence-Grounded Audio Reasoning Benchmark for Audio Language Models\n来源：arXiv\n链接：https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.39847\n摘要：Audio language models (ALMs) are increasingly used for audio deepfake detection (ADD), yet existing benchmarks assess their verdicts or rationale plausibility without verifying the underlying acoustic evidence. We further propose a bona-fide-based acoustic evidence agent (BAEA), which equips a frozen ALM with controlled acoustic tools under \\textsc{fixed} or \\textsc{adaptive} evidence-acquisition policies. Experiments with six ALMs reveal a clear gap between plausible rationales and verifiable acoustic evidence reasoning, while BAEA-"

paper = "https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.39846"
code = ""
dataset = ""
link = ""
content = "研究定义了角色能力泄漏（RCL）：模型虽然能生成符合指定角色风格的文本，却仍展现出明显超越该角色设定的实际能力。作者提出基于课程体系的RoleCapBench，覆盖六种教育角色和从小学到A-level的四个评估层级，并测试三种开放权重推理模型。实验显示，朴素角色提示能获得较高的角色语言风格得分，但模型的超角色准确率仍高达0.811至0.898，即使明确要求匹配角色能力水平也无法消除泄漏。为此，作者提出推理时干预方法Injection，将角色专属能力指南与预填充回答前缀结合，最多可将超角色准确率降低0.562，同时在多数模型上将角色内准确率损失控制在0.058以内。"
purpose = "- 为角色扮演、教育模拟和社会仿真中的模型能力一致性提供专门评测基准。\n- 揭示语言风格符合角色并不意味着底层推理能力真正受角色约束，评估时应将角色声音与角色能力分开测量。\n- Injection表明，无需重新训练即可通过能力指南和预填充前缀缓解能力泄漏，可用于构建更可信的角色智能体。\n- 该问题也提示开发者关注角色提示的构念效度，避免仅凭表面文本风格判断模型是否遵循角色设定。"
notes = "标题：When a Kindergartener Solves Calculus: Measuring Capability Leakage in Role-Prompted Reasoning Models\n来源：arXiv\n链接：https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.39846\n摘要：We investigate the problem of role-capability leakage (RCL), in which a role-prompted reasoning model generates convincing in-role text while continuing to exhibit capabilities on benchmarks that exceed those implied by the assigned role. For example, when a model is prompted to assume the role of a kindergarten student, one might expect its performance on a mathematics benchmark to reflect kindergarten-level ability rather than expert-level proficiency in solving calculus problems. We introduce RoleCapBench, a curriculum-grounded benchmark for evaluating RCL across six educational roles and four assessment levels spanning elementary school through A-level, and use it to evaluate three open-weight reasoning models. We find that although the models can generate stylistically convincing in-r"

summary = "研究发现，模型学习隐写推理显著难于隐写通信和编码推理，但在便于隐藏信息的掩护任务中仍可通过多种训练方式获得该能力。"
paper = "https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.39838"
code = ""
dataset = ""
link = ""
content = "论文比较了隐写推理、隐写通信和编码推理三种能力，并采用强化学习、上下文学习及监督微调三种方式进行诱导。多数任务中，模型只有通过监督微调才能学会隐写推理，而另外两种能力可通过全部三种方法获得。即使使用监督微调，隐写推理所需训练量也至少是隐写通信的两倍，部分模型与任务组合始终未能学会。不过，当掩护任务天然便于隐藏信息时，模型可通过三种诱导方式成功掌握隐写推理，说明该风险虽不易出现但并非遥不可及。"
purpose = "- 为思维链监控的威胁建模提供更细致的能力区分，避免将隐写通信或编码推理直接等同于隐写推理。\n- 提示安全评估应重点设计便于隐藏信息的掩护任务，因为此类环境可能显著降低隐写推理的学习门槛。\n- 可用于审计强化学习、监督微调和上下文学习流程是否意外诱导模型规避推理监控。\n- 说明思维链监控仍具有现实价值，但需要结合输出行为检测和训练过程审计等多层防护。"
notes = "标题：Learning Steganography Is Easy, Learning Steganographic Reasoning Is Hard\n来源：arXiv\n链接：https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.39838\n摘要：Chain-of-thought monitoring as an approach for AI oversight and control is threatened by the possibility of steganographic reasoning, where LLMs conceal their reasoning inside innocuous-looking text. Two neighbouring capabilities, steganographic messaging (passing a concealed message) and encoded reasoning (reasoning in an illegible but unconcealed format), have already been shown to emerge under training pressures that occur in real pipelines, such as reinforcement learning against monitors. This suggests that steganographic reasoning too might arise as an unintended side effect of training. Here, we compare how easily models learn steganographic reasoning and these two neighbouring capabilities across three elicitation methods: reinforcement learning, in-context learning, and supervised "

id = "item-64"
title = "合成预预训练可扩展，但并非源于语法先验"
subtopic = "预训练"
topics = [
    "模型训练",

summary = "合成非自然语言预预训练在最高 7B 参数和 100B 词元规模下仍能提升下游性能与词元效率，但收益更可能来自长程检索能力而非语法先验。"
paper = "https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.39827"
code = ""
dataset = ""
link = ""
content = "研究系统考察了五种预预训练（PPT）任务、四种预训练数据混合、500M 至 7B 的四档模型规模，以及最高 100B 词元的训练预算。PPT 的下游性能与词元效率收益在规模扩大后依然存在，例如在 3B 参数规模下至少可节省 21B 个预训练词元。实验未发现收益与语法可接受性在不同模型规模上保持一致，因此不支持“语法先验迁移”这一主流解释。进一步分析表明，能够改善长程检索的 PPT 任务才是主要收益来源；该效果对数据混合比例较稳健，但在完全移除网页文本后会减弱。"
purpose = "- 表明合成 PPT 可以作为成本较低的预训练前置阶段，在数十亿参数和百亿级词元预算下继续提高训练效率。\n- 提醒研究者不要仅凭下游增益推断模型获得了自然语言语法先验，应通过语法可接受性等独立指标验证机制。\n- 后续合成任务设计可优先训练长程信息检索与依赖建模能力，而非单纯模拟形式语法。\n- 在实际数据配方中应保留一定比例的网页自然语言文本，以避免 PPT 收益明显衰减。"
notes = "标题：Synthetic Pre-pretraining Survives Scale, but Not as a Grammatical Prior\n来源：arXiv\n链接：https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.39827\n摘要：Pre-pretraining (PPT) on synthetic non-natural language data improves token efficiency during language model pre-training (PT). Prior work attributes this gain to a grammatical prior, i.e., a structural inductive bias learned during PPT that transfers to natural language grammar. However, PPT has only been tested on models of at most 1B parameters and PT budgets below 2B tokens on predominantly web text. It is unknown whether PPT is effective at larger scales and under more realistic PT data mixtures that combine diverse sources (e.g., code and math). We therefore present a comprehensive study on PPT spanning five PPT tasks, four PT data mixtures, four parameter scales (500M to 7B), and PT budgets of up to 100B tokens. Our results demonstrate that the downstream performance and token effic"

summary = "FailBank 将安全模块提供的运行时反事实纠正沉淀为持久训练监督，在提升 VLA 操作成功率的同时降低策略引发的碰撞成本。"
paper = "https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.39820"
code = ""
dataset = ""
link = ""
content = "FailBank 是一个面向视觉语言动作模型的四阶段自进化框架，旨在解决基础策略与运行时安全屏障长期不一致的问题。框架使用固定的基于控制障碍函数（CBF）的安全模块作为仅观察教师，在不接管策略控制的情况下产生反事实动作纠正。结果感知的准入机制将有效建议转化为纠正目标，并保留成功且未经纠正的动作作为锚点，通过受保护的 LoRA 更新改进策略。在 VLA-Arena 的两个难度等级和两种 VLA 骨干上，FailBank 将任务成功率分别提高 8.5 和 6.9 个百分点，并将策略累计成本降低 35.6% 和 23.8%。"
purpose = "- 展示如何将临时运行时安全干预转化为可持续的策略监督与参数更新。\n- 为具身模型构建“失败收集—结果筛选—纠正训练—策略改进”的闭环自进化机制提供参考。\n- 说明保留成功原始动作作为训练锚点，有助于避免纠正数据损害模型已有能力。\n- 为兼顾机器人任务成功率与接触安全成本的后训练方法提供可复用思路。"
notes = "标题：Learning from Runtime Feedback through Failure-Bank Self-Evolution for Vision-Language-Action Models\n来源：arXiv\n链接：https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.39820\n摘要：Vision-language-action (VLA) models generalize broadly across robotic manipulation tasks, but complex environments require balancing task success with unintended contact. Runtime shields can correct individual actions, but they leave the underlying policy unchanged, so repeated disagreements may create a persistent policy-shield mismatch that blocks task progress. To address this challenge, we introduce FailBank, a four-stage self-evolving framework that converts runtime feedback into persistent policy improvement. During collection, a fixed CBF-based safety module serves as an observe-only teacher, producing counterfactual corrections while the policy remains in control. Outcome-aware admission then converts useful proposals into corrective targets and retains successful uncorrected actio"


[[items]]
id = "item-66"
title = "超越准确率：低精度快速矩阵乘法的前缀不变实现"
subtopic = "模型可靠性"
topics = [
    "人工智能可靠性",
    "系统优化",
    "模型评估",
    "推理",
    "成本优化",
]
research = []
keywords = [
    "模型可靠性",
    "系统优化",
    "FP8矩阵乘法",
    "前缀不变性",
    "Strassen算法",
]
orgs = [
    "哈尔滨工业大学",
    "复旦大学",
    "北京大学",
]
source = "arXiv"
summary = "研究揭示部分FP8快速矩阵乘法会破坏语言模型的前缀不变性，并提出可认证的两级Strassen实现，在减少块乘法的同时保证与行局部int8算子逐比特一致。"
paper = "https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.39816"
code = ""
dataset = ""
link = ""
content = "快速矩阵乘法依赖精确抵消来减少乘法，但低精度舍入会使后续token行的信息泄漏到较早输出中，从而破坏似然评分所要求的前缀不变性。在Qwen2.5-14B-Instruct的240道OpenBookQA题目上，两种表面准确率正常的FP8实现，在替换允许前缀之后的文本时分别导致5.83%和10.00%的答案发生变化，而bf16模型及已部署的行局部FP8内核均未出现变化。两级Strassen的512种符号变体虽然具有相同的常规稳定性指标，但教师强制困惑度最多相差772.4倍，说明准确率与现有稳定性标准不足以鉴别实现风险。作者构造了基于有界整数编码的可认证两级Strassen实现，先逐行量化token、再精确混合与抵消，以49次块乘法替代64次，并保证与指定行局部经典int8算子逐比特一致。"
purpose = "- 提醒低精度推理系统不能仅用任务准确率或困惑度验证数值实现，还应直接测试前缀不变性等语义级性质。\n- 为多选题似然评分、语言模型评测及缓存推理提供防止未来token信息泄漏的可验证实现方案。\n- 展示通过逐行量化和整数域精确抵消，可以兼顾Strassen矩阵乘法的计算节省与结果可靠性。\n- 认证后不同快速矩阵乘法实现只影响运行成本，不再改变任何被评分的似然值，有助于安全部署低精度算子。"
notes = "标题：Beyond Accuracy: Prefix-Invariant Realizations of Low-Precision Fast Matrix Multiplication\n来源：arXiv\n链接：https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.39816\n摘要：Fast matrix multiplication saves multiplications through exact cancellation, but rounding sums that mix token rows can leave contributions from later tokens in earlier language model outputs. This threatens prefix invariance, which multiple-choice likelihood scoring relies on: a scored likelihood must depend only on its allowed prefix. On Qwen2.5-14B-Instruct, two fast FP8 realizations repaired to ordinary-looking accuracy still change the answers chosen by likelihood on 5.83% and 10.00% of 240 OpenBookQA items when only the text after the allowed prefix is replaced with the bf16 model's own greedy continuation. Both row-local controls, the bf16 model and a deployed FP8 matrix multiplication kernel, change none. Accuracy thus does not certify prefix invariance, and the stability criteria w"


[[items]]
id = "pilot"
title = "PILOT：由预测误差伪标签触发的在线时序模型重训练"
subtopic = "持续学习"
topics = [
    "持续学习",
    "时序建模",
    "在线学习",

]

    "首尔大学",
]
source = "arXiv"
summary = "PILOT利用未来预测误差增幅构造伪标签，从已观测误差状态中学习在线重训练时机，在预测性能与部署成本之间取得更优平衡。"
paper = "https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.39789"
code = ""
dataset = ""
link = ""
content = "PILOT面向非平稳数据流中的在线时间序列预测，解决资源有限条件下“何时重训练”的决策问题。它不依赖漂移告警或模型陈旧度等间接指标，而是使用已完成预测的真实误差作为直接部署反馈。由于缺少重训练时机的真实标签，框架根据未来预测误差的上升构造伪标签，并训练轻量级评分器从当前误差状态预测是否需要重训练。该方法可作为插件接入DLinear、iTransformer和TimesNet等任意预测骨干，无需修改模型架构；在八个基准上的实验显示，其平均排名和性能—效率权衡均具有优势。"
purpose = "- 为非平稳环境中的预测系统提供数据驱动的重训练触发机制，减少固定周期重训练造成的计算浪费。\n- 说明预测误差本身可作为比漂移告警更直接的闭环反馈信号，用于持续学习与模型运维。\n- 伪标签机制可用于缺少最优触发决策标注的场景，为其他在线模型更新任务提供参考。\n- 插件式设计便于在不改动预测骨干的情况下部署，并可按资源预算调节性能与重训练成本。"
notes = "标题：Pseudo-Label-Triggered Retraining from Forecast Errors for Online Time Series Forecasting\n来源：arXiv\n链接：https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.39789\n摘要：Real-world time series forecasting systems operate under non-stationary data streams, where forecasting performance may degrade over time. Although retraining can recover the performance, it incurs non-trivial computational and operational costs. Under limited deployment resources, the key challenge is therefore not only how to retrain but also when to retrain. While existing retraining policies often rely on indirect indicators such as drift alarms or model staleness, we instead use realized forecast errors as direct deployment feedback. In this paper, we propose PILOT (Pseudo-label-Informed Learned Online Trigger), an online retraining framework that learns when to retrain from forecast-error dynamics. Since ground-truth retraining labels are unavailable, PILOT constructs a pseudo-label "


[[items]]
id = "item-68"
title = "多智能体潜空间通信的安全风险"
subtopic = "多智能体协作风险"
topics = [
    "多智能体协作安全",
    "多智能体系统",
    "人工智能可靠性",
    "安全对齐",
    "强化学习",
]
research = []
keywords = [
    "多智能体协作风险",
    "多智能体系统",
    "潜空间通信",
    "安全对齐",
    "强化学习",
]
source = "arXiv"
summary = "研究发现，潜空间通信链路可能在不改变已完成安全对齐的底层智能体时显著提高有害请求服从率，但也可通过安全奖励适配修复受损链路。"
paper = "https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.39788"
code = ""
dataset = ""
link = ""
content = "潜空间通信通过轻量可训练链路直接传递智能体内部表征，可降低文本通信的词元、计算与延迟开销。实验表明，即使仅使用良性数据训练链路，也可能使多智能体系统比文本通信更容易服从有害请求。攻击者可通过有害样本监督优化、良性训练数据投毒或兼顾任务效用与有害服从的强化学习奖励进一步放大风险；后者在三种通信拓扑和四项安全基准上将平均有害服从分数从 27.9 提升至 76.9。研究还发现，无需更新底层智能体，只需将链路奖励调整为安全目标，便可显著降低各类攻击造成的有害服从。"
purpose = "- 提醒安全评估不能只检查单个已对齐模型，还应覆盖通信链路、拓扑结构和整个多智能体系统。\n- 潜空间接口应被视为独立攻击面，对链路训练数据、奖励函数和参数更新实施审计与访问控制。\n- 可借鉴论文的链路级安全奖励方法，在不重新训练底层智能体的情况下低成本修复受损系统。\n- 部署潜空间通信前，应同时测试有害服从率与良性任务效用，避免效率优化隐性削弱安全对齐。"
notes = "标题：Safety of Latent Communication in Multi-Agent Systems\n来源：arXiv\n链接：https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.39788\n摘要：Latent communication enables multi-agent systems to exchange information directly in internal representation space, reducing the token, computation, and latency overhead of text-based communication. To this end, lightweight trainable links are introduced to map the sender's representations into the receiver's input space. In this work, we show that even benign link training can increase harmful compliance relative to text-based communication while the underlying safety-aligned agents remain unchanged. An attacker can amplify this effect by optimizing the links on harmful query--response pairs or poisoning otherwise benign training data. We further develop a reinforcement-learning attack that rewards harmful compliance alongside benign task performance without requiring harmful target respo"


[[items]]
id = "graphmas"
title = "GraphMAS：图学习多智能体协作系统化基准"
subtopic = "多智能体系统"
topics = [

keywords = [

    "专长分解",
]

    "New York University Shanghai",
    "纽约大学",
    "Northwestern University",
]
source = "arXiv"
summary = "GraphMAS 通过统一评测多种多智能体协调范式，表明专长分解与实例自适应智能体选择能够有效提升图学习的准确率和效率。"
paper = "https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.39777"
code = ""
dataset = ""
link = ""
content = "GraphMAS 面向图学习构建了一个系统化多智能体协调基准，以共享的图推理专家池提供局部、长程、全局结构及语义等异构视角。基准从智能体间交互和运行时自适应两个维度组织四类范式、七种代表性协调方法，并在三个领域的七个文本属性图及两类图学习任务上统一评测。实验发现，协调多个专家优于单个专家和单智能体图推理，其收益主要来自专家间的推理分解，而非简单扩大可访问证据。更复杂的智能体交互并不稳定有效，实例自适应的专家选择则实现了最佳准确率—效率权衡，且学习到的协调策略能够迁移至未见图。"
purpose = "- 为比较图学习中的多智能体聚合、交互和自适应控制方法提供统一评测框架。\n- 提示系统设计应优先利用异构专家的互补性与任务分解，而非盲目增加交互复杂度。\n- 实例级动态专家选择可作为兼顾预测准确率与推理成本的关键机制。\n- 固定专家池上的可学习协调策略具备跨图迁移潜力，可用于构建更通用的图推理系统。"
notes = "标题：GraphMAS: A Systematic Benchmark of Multi-Agent Coordination for Graph Learning\n来源：arXiv\n链接：https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.39777\n摘要：LLM-based multi-agent systems coordinate specialized reasoning through aggregation, interaction, and adaptive control, yet their potential for graph learning remains unexplored. Graph learning is a natural setting for such systems because useful evidence may arise from heterogeneous local, long-range, global structural, and semantic perspectives whose relevance varies across instances. Existing LLM-based graph learning approaches primarily rely on single-agent reasoning, while multi-agent coordination has been studied mainly in general reasoning settings. Consequently, it remains unclear whether multiple specialized agents can improve graph learning and how coordination strategies should be designed and evaluated. To address this gap, we introduce GraphMAS, a systematic benchmark of multi-"


[[items]]
id = "memcodex"
title = "MemCodex：可自编程演化的分层智能体记忆"
subtopic = "长期记忆"
topics = [
    "智能体记忆",
    "自演化",

]
research = [

    "程序进化",

    "上下文压缩",
]
orgs = [
    "Mila",
    "Université de Montréal",
]
source = "arXiv"
summary = "MemCodex 将经验组织为可执行的分层记忆程序，并通过开放式程序演化自主改写各层的构建、索引、检索与路由机制，以更低上下文开销提升智能体任务表现。"
paper = "https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.39765"
code = ""
dataset = ""
link = ""
content = "MemCodex 是一种自演化分层记忆系统，将智能体经验组织为摘要、关系知识、可复用技能和潜在记忆等可执行程序。其开放式程序演化不局限于预定义组件组合，而是改写每层记忆的构建、索引、检索和路由方式，同时优化层内实现与跨层组合。查询时，系统由粗到细遍历记忆层级，在证据充分时提前停止，必要时再下钻至原始历史。配套的 MemArena 运行时通过统一接口承载异构数据与记忆系统；实验中，MemCodex 相对最强自适应记忆基线将平均任务成功率提升 10.1%，上下文词元减少至约 1/3.4，推理速度提升 2.1 倍。"
purpose = "- 为不同复杂度的查询动态选择记忆粒度，避免固定记忆工作流造成检索不足或上下文浪费。\n- 表明记忆系统的构建、索引、检索与路由均可作为开放式程序演化对象，为智能体记忆自优化提供新路径。\n- 分层读取与证据充分即停止的机制可用于降低长程智能体的上下文成本和推理延迟。\n- MemArena 的统一接口有助于公平比较异构记忆方案，并支持记忆组件的快速替换与组合实验。"
notes = "标题：MemCodex: Self-Programming Hierarchical Memory for Language Agents\n来源：arXiv\n链接：https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.39765\n摘要：Agent memory faces heterogeneous access needs: a single-hop question may require one piece of evidence, whereas a multi-hop question must combine evidence from multiple sources. Predefined memory workflows cannot adapt to these varying needs. Recent adaptive methods search or learn over memory components and their compositions, but the design space itself remains predefined. We introduce MemCodex, a self-evolving hierarchical memory system that organizes experience into executable memory programs for summaries, relational knowledge, reusable skills, and latent memory. Open-ended program evolution searches the open design space of layer programs by rewriting how each layer is constructed, indexed, retrieved, and routed, thereby adapting both within-layer implementations and cross-layer comp"


[[items]]
id = "grgc"
title = "GRGC：校准组内奖励几何的在策略黑盒蒸馏"
subtopic = "在策略蒸馏"
topics = [

research = []

]

    "浙江大学",
]
source = "arXiv"
summary = "论文提出组内奖励几何调节框架 GRGC，通过校准评论器奖励分布并增强组内间隔，为 GRPO 构造更稳定有效的优势信号。"
paper = "https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.39757"
code = "https://github.com/2018cx/GRGC"
dataset = ""
link = ""
content = "黑盒蒸馏可将仅提供文本输出的 API 教师模型能力迁移至较小的学生模型，但现有 GAD 方法的评论器训练目标与 GRPO 所需的组内相对奖励存在错配。该错配可能导致同一提示下的奖励尺度坍缩或间隔脆弱，使优势估计和策略优化不稳定。GRGC 在评论器侧使用高斯组内最优传输校准，使排序后的奖励匹配以组中心为基准的高斯分位数，从而维持非坍缩的分布范围和平滑间隔。在策略侧，组幂调制在保留奖励排序的同时扩大与优化相关的间隔；跨教师、学生模型系列、模型规模及数据集的实验显示，该方法改善了分布内外表现，且相对 GAD 仅增加可忽略的开销。"
purpose = "- 为基于 GRPO 的黑盒蒸馏提供更稳定、更具区分度的组内优势信号。\n- 揭示评论器的教师—学生判别目标与学生样本组内排序需求之间的关键错配。\n- 可将奖励几何校准作为低开销模块，应用于对抗蒸馏及其他依赖组相对优势的强化学习训练流程。\n- 提示奖励模型不仅需要正确排序样本，还应显式控制组内尺度、间隔和平滑性。"
notes = "标题：Revisiting On-policy Adversarial Black-Box Distillation: Calibrating Groupwise Reward Geometry for Effective Advantage Construction\n来源：arXiv\n链接：https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.39757\n摘要：Black-box distillation is a practical route for transferring capabilities from API-accessible large language models that expose only text outputs into smaller student models. Recent on-policy adversarial methods such as GAD improve over SeqKD by forming an adversarial loop between a critic and a student, where the critic provides rewards for GRPO-based student policy optimization over the student's sampled responses. However, GRPO computes advantages from the within-group relative rewards of student samples for the same prompt, whereas the critic is trained primarily to distinguish teacher responses from student responses. This objective mismatch can produce reward groups with collapsed scale or fragile margins, leading to brittle grouped optimization signals. We propose Groupwise Reward G"

content = "LatentHarness 将长输入中的证据保留和多步推理计算建模为序列化的潜在动作选择。模型在每个内部步骤可选择 THINK 继续计算、RECALL 从快速权重记忆中读取输入证据或中间状态，以及 EXIT 输出下一个词元。训练时，反事实策略蒸馏会对每种动作进行单步分支，并根据其对输出词元的影响为策略提供监督，同时借助反事实回忆梯度学习应保留哪些中间状态。在六个通用及长上下文推理基准上，1.4B 模型相对最强基线分别提升 2.8% 和 10.0%，运行速度达到最强长上下文基线的 5.9 倍。"
purpose = "- 为统一设计模型内部记忆访问与潜在计算提供离散动作接口，避免将长程记忆和推理扩展割裂处理。\n- 反事实动作分支可用于细粒度信用分配，直接评估继续思考、调用记忆或立即输出的边际价值。\n- 快速权重记忆与选择性状态保留有望降低长上下文推理的计算成本，并提升关键信息的持续可访问性。\n- THINK、RECALL、EXIT 策略可为测试时计算预算控制、推理早停和动态记忆调度提供参考。"
notes = "标题：LatentHarness: Learning Latent Actions for Memory and Reasoning via Counterfactual Policy Distillation\n来源：arXiv\n链接：https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.39740\n摘要：Long-context reasoning faces two complementary bottlenecks: retaining evidence across long inputs and sustaining computation across many reasoning steps. Existing approaches largely address them separately, with external memory extending access to distant evidence and latent reasoning compressing multi-step computation. We introduce LatentHarness, which unifies memory access and latent reasoning as sequential latent action selection. At each internal step, the model chooses THINK for further computation, RECALL from a fast-weight memory of input evidence and intermediate reasoning states, or EXIT to emit the next token. We train this policy with counterfactual policy distillation, which branches every action for one step and scores its effect on the emitted token. These gains teach the pol"

code = ""

link = ""
content = "OverForge是一种免训练的分层智能体架构，将持久的角色分工与协作策略同具体动作层面的战术推理解耦。每个智能体维护私有的、以伙伴为条件的世界模型，并由元认知“前额叶皮层模块”构造策略—动作分支、预测后果，在置信度足够时执行决策。在OvercookedV2连通厨房中，OverForge完成7份汤，而各平面式LLM基线仅完成3份，同时能够保持约定角色并接受陌生伙伴提出的新角色。消融实验与固定策略探针表明，持久策略能够引导战术适应，跨回合伙伴知识则有助于提高任务表现和伙伴行为预测能力。"
purpose = "- 为长期多智能体协作提供策略层与动作层解耦的可解释架构，避免直接从观察映射到动作造成的短视决策。\n- 可借鉴伙伴条件世界模型，对不同协作者的惯例、角色偏好和行为模式进行持续建模。\n- 表明无需额外训练也可通过前向想象、置信度判断和跨回合记忆增强开放环境中的协作适应。\n- 适用于协作游戏、具身任务及动态团队编排等需要稳定分工与即时调整的场景。"
notes = "标题：OverForge: Reasoning Through Strategies and Tactics Helps Cooperative Lifelong Adaptation\n来源：arXiv\n链接：https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.39727\n摘要：Cooperative language-model agents must coordinate over long horizons and adapt to changing environments and to partners with unfamiliar conventions, yet existing agents map observations to actions without separating persistent coordination strategies from their tactical execution. We introduce OverForge, a training-free hierarchical architecture that separates strategic reasoning over roles and divisions of labour from tactical reasoning over actions within each agent's private, partner-conditioned world model. A metacognitive Prefrontal Cortex Module couples the two levels by forming strategy-action branches, imagining their consequences with a forward model, and committing when confident. In OvercookedV2, OverForge delivers 7 soups in a connected kitchen versus 3 for each flat LLM baseli"


[[items]]

summary = "ArchitectureIQ 通过让模型和人类根据合成数据集预测最佳训练方案，评估其模型训练直觉，并揭示当前大模型在架构推断、结构化理解和数据敏感性方面的局限。"
paper = "https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.39714"
code = ""
dataset = ""
link = ""
content = "ArchitectureIQ 的每道题给出一个合成数据集和若干训练方案，要求受试者预测哪种方案能取得最佳测试指标。前沿模型整体准确率约为 76%，高于最佳人类研究者的 66%，但在仅涉及架构的问题上，最佳人类达到 65%，GPT-6 Astra 仅为 38%。增加思维链计算并未显著改善成绩，表明模型的训练直觉更偏经验性，尚未形成可支持结构化推理的“AI 科学”语言。研究还发现，仅含 20 条知识的压缩知识库便能显著提升较弱模型，而模型对数据集属性普遍不够敏感，说明对数据规律的理解仍是关键短板。"
purpose = "- 为比较大模型与人类研究者的模型训练直觉提供专门评测基准。\n- 可用于诊断模型对架构选择、训练配方和数据属性之间关系的理解缺陷。\n- 结果提示单纯增加思维链预算未必有效，应加强结构化训练知识表示与可复用知识库建设。\n- 模型选择研究应更加重视数据集属性，避免依赖脱离数据特征的通用经验法则。"
notes = "标题：ArchitectureIQ: On the Measure of Training Intuition\n来源：arXiv\n链接：https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.39714\n摘要：Top researchers have good intuition, but do language models have as good intuition about model training as top AI researchers? To measure model intuition of LLMs and humans, we introduce the ArchitectureIQ benchmark. Each question presents a synthetic dataset and several training recipes, and the test-taker is asked to predict the recipe yielding the best test metric. Overall, we find that LLMs' model intuition is good but has four limitations: (1) The intuition is imperfect, or even sub-human in some cases. Frontier models achieve around 76% accuracy (random choice 33%) vs best human researcher (66.0%), yet remain far from perfect. For architecture-only questions, best human achieves 65% while GPT-6 Astra only has 38%. (2) The intuition is empirical, not structured, supported by the fact "

content = "研究发现，特权教师附近的局部参数扰动能够产生互补的、与参考答案一致的修正，其联合覆盖范围超过未经扰动的单一教师。N-OPSD离线采用贪心策略，根据各参考位置上经过过滤的参考词元增益，筛选出紧凑的冻结专家池。在线阶段通过MaxPeak确定锚点词元，再以分位数策略从首选词元一致的专家中选择监督分布，并使用截断前向KL目标训练学生。在Qwen3-1.7B、4B和8B上，该方法相对标准OPSD将AIME 2024、AIME 2025与HMMT 2025三项基准的Average@12分别提高2.75、1.67和1.94个百分点，推理时仅需蒸馏后的学生模型。"
purpose = "- 表明单一教师参数并非最优监督来源，可通过局部参数邻域挖掘互补的推理纠错信号。\n- 为在策略蒸馏提供“离线专家池选择、在线按状态路由”的可复用训练范式。\n- 专家选择应兼顾参考词元增益、专家间覆盖重叠及当前学生状态，而非仅选择峰值置信度最高的教师。\n- 多专家监督仅用于训练，部署阶段无需增加推理模型数量或路由成本。"
notes = "标题：Better Supervision Is Nearby: Neighborhood On-Policy Self-Distillation\n来源：arXiv\n链接：https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.39687\n摘要：On-policy self-distillation (OPSD) trains mathematical reasoning models using a privileged teacher that sees a reference solution and supervises student-sampled prefixes. Standard OPSD uses one fixed parameter setting at every state, but nearby settings may offer additional supervision. We find that local parameter perturbations reveal complementary reference-aligned corrections under the same reference context. Different experts supply these corrections at different reference positions. Their pool covers more such positions than the unperturbed privileged teacher. We introduce Neighborhood OPSD (N-OPSD) to turn these corrections into supervision at student-visited states. Offline, greedy selection builds a compact pool of frozen experts by rewarding filtered reference-token gains beyond t"

id = "robocoach"
title = "RoboCoach：世界模型主动指导组合式机器人技能改进"

summary = "RoboCoach通过世界模型预演组合任务失败，主动选择应补充示范的子任务和应更新的技能专家，以较少数据显著提升长程机器人操作能力。"

code = ""

link = ""
content = "RoboCoach提出Route-Imagine-Diagnose-Improve（RIDI）闭环，在共享的动作条件世界模型COACHWORLD中执行可复用技能专家并预演任务组合。进度评判器定位首个未完成的子任务，汇总失败记录后决定应请求哪些子任务示范，以及更新哪些专家适配器。想象成功率与真实部署成功率在22个任务—策略组合上高度相关（ρ=0.840），且方法在相同数据预算和更新计划下优于对照基线。仅增加150条子任务示范，Franka平台成功率由13.3%升至75.0%，AgileX平台由40.0%升至83.8%，并能迁移到未见过的任务组合。"
purpose = "- 展示世界模型不仅可用于规划，还可作为主动教练，将想象中的失败转化为有针对性的监督信号。\n- 通过定位首个失败子任务并局部更新技能专家，降低长程机器人任务依赖端到端示范的成本。\n- 为模块化具身策略的持续改进提供闭环范式，可用于示范采集、专家路由和适配器更新。\n- 结果表明，基于失败诊断的主动数据选择有助于提升组合泛化和跨任务技能复用。"
notes = "标题：RoboCoach: World Models as Active Coaches for Compositional Robot Skills\n来源：arXiv\n链接：https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.39685\n摘要：Long-horizon robot manipulation reuses skills across many task compositions, but improving these compositions with additional end-to-end demonstrations is costly. A practical self-improving system must decide both what to teach next and where to apply that supervision. We present ROBOCOACH, a world-model-guided coaching framework that uses imagined failures to guide demonstration requests and expert updates. Its Route-Imagine-Diagnose-Improve (RIDI) loop executes reusable skill experts inside COACHWORLD, our shared action-conditioned world model, and uses a progress judge to record the first subtask that fails to complete. Aggregated records select which subtask demonstrations to acquire and which expert adapters to update. Across two simulation suites and two real-robot platforms, imagine"

title = "SEPAL：分离专家对与答案级融合提升 LLM 协作可靠性"
subtopic = "多智能体协作框架"
topics = [

summary = "SEPAL 通过三个相互隔离、角色专门化的 Actor-Critic 团队独立推理和修正，并仅在答案层多数投票，从而兼顾反馈优化、候选多样性与协作可靠性。"
paper = "https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.39645"
code = "https://github.com/zhansan114514/SEPAL"
dataset = ""
link = ""
content = "SEPAL 设置三个私有 Actor-Critic 团队，分别负责直接推理、证据落地与答案验证，并通过角色特定训练形成差异化推理目标。每个 Critic 只在本团队内部指导 Actor 修订，避免共享讨论将同一错误传播给其他候选。修订完成后，系统仅对最终答案进行多数投票，在决策前始终隔离各团队的推理历史。实验覆盖五个开放权重模型和五个问答基准，相较匹配的单 Actor-Critic 对平均准确率提高 1.81 个百分点，且所有骨干模型均有提升。"
purpose = "- 为多智能体协作提供“过程隔离、答案融合”的设计范式，降低错误共识和跨候选污染。\n- 说明角色专门化训练能够产生比单纯重复采样更具功能差异的候选答案。\n- 可用于构建可靠问答、证据核验和高风险决策系统，在保留内部反馈能力的同时维持集成多样性。\n- 后续可进一步研究团队数量、角色分工与投票规则对准确率、成本和错误相关性的影响。"
notes = "标题：SEPAL: Separated Expert Pairs with Answer-Level Fusion for Reliable LLM Collaboration\n来源：arXiv\n链接：https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.39645\n摘要：Multi-agent collaboration lets large language models (LLMs) improve question answering through deliberation and feedback. Yet shared discussion couples correction with exposure to the same mistakes, which can erode the diversity needed for voting. Self-consistency offers sampling diversity without feedback, while single-pair Actor-Critic collaboration refines only one candidate. We introduce SEPAL, which assigns three private Actor-Critic teams to direct reasoning, evidence grounding, and verification. Role-specific training gives the teams different reasoning objectives beyond sampling variation. Each Critic guides revisions within its own team, preventing feedback from carrying errors across candidates. Once revision ends, majority voting combines only the final answers, keeping the reas"

summary = "研究发现序列测试时扩展常因过早陷入答案吸引子而停止改进，并提出模型混合干预以增强探索、扩大解空间覆盖率并提升递归自聚合准确率。"
paper = "https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.39632"
code = ""

content = "论文比较了并行与序列测试时扩展：并行方法独立采样多个答案，而序列方法基于先前答案迭代改进，但两者在长时间尺度下都容易停止增益。作者将序列扩展的瓶颈归因于“吸引子”，即轨迹进入一组相似答案后难以继续探索其他解；在27种方法、模型与基准组合中，53.8%的序列轨迹会在四轮内进入吸引子。为此，论文提出简单的模型混合干预，使平均吸引子命中率降低21.2个百分点，并实现超越同等计算量并行基线的解覆盖率。该方法还将递归自聚合的准确率至少提高2.2个百分点，表明长程测试时扩展应更重视可持续探索的序列式改进。"
purpose = "- 为序列测试时计算提供失效机制解释，可通过监测答案多样性和轨迹收敛情况识别吸引子。\n- 模型混合可作为低复杂度的探索增强手段，帮助推理轨迹跳出局部答案簇。\n- 设计长程推理系统时，不应只增加迭代轮数，还需显式平衡答案继承与新解探索。\n- 评估测试时扩展方法时，可同时报告准确率、解覆盖率、吸引子命中率及单位计算收益。"
notes = "标题：Towards Better Exploration in Sequential Test-Time Scaling\n来源：arXiv\n链接：https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.39632\n摘要：Test-time scaling improves language model reasoning by spending additional compute at inference. However, both classes of existing methods often fail to continue improving over long timescales. Parallel methods repeatedly sample independent answers from the model, scaling poorly on problems the model is unlikely to solve in a single attempt. In contrast, sequential methods build on previous answers to access new ideas, yet so far have not been shown to reach answers beyond those found by parallel scaling. First, we show that sequential scaling often stops improving because it becomes prematurely trapped in an attractor: a set of answers that prevents exploration of different answers once entered. Across 27 combinations of scaling methods, models, and benchmarks, we find that 53.8% of seque"

content = "论文研究资格审查、合同审核等规则治理场景中的证据关键性判断，即识别哪些证据一旦被误读或遗漏便可能逆转最终决策。InterPact使用冻结语言模型编辑案例事实，同时固定规则与非目标条件，构造反事实训练对，并由人工审核条件变化和决策变化标签。训练时，传播验证器将证据对应的条件概率与条件化决策预测通过固定组合操作结合，把决策变化监督传播至基础模型。推理阶段无需人工或更强模型介入，基础模型可直接依据原始案例和目标证据判断其关键性；该方法取得68.28%的准确率，优于六种基线。"
purpose = "- 为规则驱动的高风险决策提供证据检查优先级，使有限审核资源集中于可能改变结论的关键证据。\n- 将反事实干预与显式规则组合结合，可用于追踪“证据—条件—决策”的影响传播路径。\n- 可应用于资格认定、合同审查、合规审核等需要可靠条件判断和决策验证的场景。\n- 说明决策敏感性学习可作为提升语言模型规则推理安全性与可靠性的基础机制。"
notes = "标题：Is This Evidence Decision-Critical? Learning to Verify Rule-Governed Decisions\n来源：arXiv\n链接：https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.39608\n摘要：Rule-based reasoning, as in eligibility checks and contract reviews, requires language models to assess evidence against individual conditions and combine their judgments under explicit rules. Errors in evidence assessment can leave a decision unchanged, but misinterpreting or overlooking decision-critical evidence can reverse it. Identifying such evidence allows more capable models to focus on checking the corresponding condition judgments, supporting accurate and safe decisions. Recognizing the evidence's criticality requires understanding how evidence affects a condition judgment and how that judgment affects the decision. To achieve the goal, we propose a INTERvention-based imPACT learning framework (InterPact), which enables counterfactual verification of evidence criticality in rule-"


[[items]]
id = "pretext-ai"
title = "Pretext：绕过 AI 智能体恶意技能检测框架"
subtopic = "漏洞挖掘"

research = []

summary = "白盒攻击框架 Pretext 可迭代生成兼具正常功能与恶意载荷的智能体技能，对固定及协同适应检测器的绕过率最高分别达到 97% 和 77%。"
paper = "https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.39607"
code = ""
dataset = ""
link = ""
content = "智能体技能通过向上下文注入指令和信息扩展能力，但第三方技能市场也可能借此向受害智能体植入恶意行为。现有防御通常结合确定性静态检查与基于 LLM 的语义判定，例如 NVIDIA SkillSpector。Pretext 在已知检测器的白盒条件下迭代改写技能，将恶意载荷由代码迁移至自然语言，并通过合法用途包装及跨文件拆分指令规避检测。针对三个开源模型，Pretext 对固定检测器和协同适应检测器的最高攻击成功率分别达到 97% 和 77%，暴露出当前技能扫描机制的明显缺陷。"
purpose = "- 为智能体技能市场和安装流程的安全审计提供高成功率的对抗测试方法。\n- 说明仅组合静态分析与单次 LLM 语义判定，难以识别自然语言载荷和跨文件分散攻击。\n- 启示防御系统引入跨文件联合分析、运行时行为监控、权限隔离及对抗性持续评测。\n- 提醒技能扫描器应按攻击者知晓检测逻辑的白盒威胁模型进行设计和验证。"
notes = "标题：Pretext: Defeating Malicious Skill Detection Frameworks for AI Agents\n来源：arXiv\n链接：https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.39607\n摘要：Skills extend an agent's capabilities by injecting instructions and information into the context, and are widely used by agents such as OpenClaw and Claude Code. Prior work shows third-party marketplaces host malicious skills that give attackers direct influence over the victim's agent. The emerging defense scans skills before installation, pairing deterministic static checks with an LLM-based semantic judge, as in NVIDIA's SkillSpector. We show that such defenses fall to an attacker who knows the detector. Our white-box LLM attacker, Pretext, iteratively crafts skills that evade detection while still delivering the payload and performing the benign task: moving the payload from code into natural language leaves static analysis inert, while framing it as the skill's legitimate purpose and "

paper = "https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.39568"

content = "论文提出VeriCodeBench，要求大模型仅依赖自己生成的形式规约和代码完成验证，以弥补使用预言规约进行分阶段评测的局限。基准包含400道覆盖C、Java、Rust和Python的语言原生问题，并围绕软件开发中的实际需求评估规约覆盖率、代码有效性及问题级联合成功率。作者进一步提出CodeNova，通过约束引导的规约生成明确需求，再利用验证器反馈进行针对性的实现修复。实验表明，自生成规约是端到端可验证代码生成的主要瓶颈，更复杂的规约并不必然提高验证成功率；CodeNova则在各项指标上均带来明显改善。"
purpose = "- 为可验证代码生成提供更贴近真实开发流程的端到端评测基准，避免预言规约掩盖系统级失败。\n- 可用于分别诊断需求形式化、代码生成和验证修复环节的能力瓶颈。\n- 说明规约复杂度并非越高越好，后续系统应同时优化规约覆盖、可实现性与验证友好性。\n- 展示了将约束引导与验证器反馈闭环结合，用于提高代码可靠性和自动修复效率的可行路径。"
notes = "标题：Self-Spec Verifiable Code Generation\n来源：arXiv\n链接：https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.39568\n摘要：Large language models (LLMs) may generate unreliable code on corner cases missed by testing, while formal verification can provide machine-checkable guarantees. Recently, researchers have proposed several benchmarks to evaluate the capabilities of LLMs in generating formally verifiable code, where LLMs need to formulate formal specifications, generate the corresponding code, and verify its correctness. However, existing benchmarks have two key limitations: (I) They primarily evaluate specification and code generation stage-wise, with code generation typically conditioned on an oracle specification. This setup overlooks whether strong stage-wise performance translates into end-to-end success. (II)They mainly focus on a single proof-oriented language and mathematically structured tasks, offe"


[[items]]
id = "acr"
title = "ACR：溯因学习中的候选保留策略"

summary = "论文提出溯因候选保留方法 ACR，通过联合权衡保留候选的不确定性与被丢弃的模型概率质量，降低伪标签监督误差。"
paper = "https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.39561"
code = ""

content = "溯因学习利用符号目标的候选解释监督神经感知模型，但多个有效解释可能为同一输入分配冲突标签。论文将坐标级监督误差界分解为保留不确定性、丢弃的模型概率质量和模型失配，其中前两项可由候选保留集合控制。作者提出 Abductive Candidate Retention（ACR），以贪心方式加入候选，仅在其恢复的概率质量超过新增不确定性时接受。实验显示，ACR 在多数聚合模加法设置中优于单候选基线与 A3BL，消融实验也验证了联合考虑不确定性和后验质量的必要性。"
purpose = "- 为存在多种有效符号解释的神经符号学习任务提供比单一伪标签更稳健的监督策略。\n- 可将“不确定性—概率质量”权衡用于候选解释、伪标签或推理轨迹的筛选。\n- 误差界为候选集合的保留与裁剪提供理论依据，有助于减少错误标签强化和监督信号扩散。"
notes = "标题：Candidate Retention for Abductive Learning\n来源：arXiv\n链接：https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.39561\n摘要：Abductive learning combines neural perception with symbolic reasoning, using explanations generated by abduction to supervise the perception model. Multiple valid explanations of the same symbolic target can assign conflicting labels to the same inputs. Common policies select a single candidate as a pseudo-label, which may reinforce mistaken assignments, or weight all candidates, which may spread supervision across competing labels. These risks motivate selecting a retained subset to balance supervision sharpness and model-mass coverage. To guide this choice, we bound the coordinate-level supervision error using retained uncertainty, discarded model mass, and model mismatch. For a fixed model and training pair, only the first two terms depend on the retained set. We propose Abductive Candi"


[[items]]
id = "item-83"
title = "扩散语言模型的自排斥采样"
subtopic = "采样控制"
topics = [
    "扩散模型",

    "推理",

]
orgs = [
    "ETH Zurich",
    "École Polytechnique Fédérale de Lausanne",
]
source = "arXiv"
summary = "Self-Repulsion 通过在并行去噪路径间施加词元重复惩罚，无需训练或额外模型调用即可提升扩散语言模型的答案多样性与投票准确率。"
paper = "https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.39560"
code = ""
dataset = ""
link = ""
content = "论文提出面向掩码扩散语言模型的自排斥采样（Self-Repulsion, SR），利用同批路径已经提交的词元选择，对相同位置上的重复词元降低 logit。多条路径共享一次批处理前向计算，但按顺序提交选择，使后续路径能够感知同一步内先前路径的决定；该方法无需训练，也不增加前向或反向计算次数，并可在温度为零时产生不同路径。当所有路径基于相同 logits 同时提交某个位置时，该更新等价于最大化总 logit 减去凸重复成本。在 LLaDA-8B-Instruct 上，10 条路径、128 个去噪步骤的确定性 SR 在 GSM8K 达到 80.38% 多数投票准确率，高于普通贪心解码的 70.17%；GSM8K、MATH 和 TruthfulQA 实验表明，收益主要来自正确答案覆盖率的提高。"
purpose = "- 为扩散语言模型提供无需训练和额外模型调用的多样化解码方法，可直接嵌入多样本投票流程。\n- 表明通过路径间显式抑制重复，可以在不依赖升温采样的情况下兼顾单样本质量与候选覆盖率。\n- 可将重复惩罚视为批内候选协同选择目标，为并行采样、推理预算分配和自一致性优化提供新思路。\n- 实际应用中需按任务与解码机制调节惩罚强度，因为不同基准和扩散模式下的收益存在明显差异。"
notes = "标题：Self-Repulsive Sampling for Diffusion Language Models\n来源：arXiv\n链接：https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.39560\n摘要：Sampling several responses and voting over their answers can improve a language model's accuracy, but repeated answers limit the benefit of additional samples. Raising temperature increases diversity at a potential cost to per-sample accuracy. We introduce Self-Repulsion (SR), a sampler for masked diffusion language models that uses peer commitments to diversify the pool. At each penalized denoising step, each path lowers a token's logit according to how many peers have committed that token at the same position. Paths share a batched forward pass and then commit in sequence, so later paths observe choices made earlier in the same step. This coupling requires no training or additional forward or backward pass and can produce distinct paths even at temperature zero. When all paths commit a p"

id = "rankevolve"
title = "RankEvolve：可靠的多智能体排序模型自动研究框架"
subtopic = "可信执行"
topics = [
    "自动化科学研究",
    "多智能体系统",

]
source = "arXiv"
summary = "RankEvolve通过可执行操作协议、运行时状态机和异构编程智能体互审机制，提高长周期自动研究中模型实验的执行准确性与迭代可靠性。"
paper = "https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.39551"
code = ""
dataset = ""
link = ""
content = "RankEvolve面向生成式排序模型的自动演化，将实验阶段、门控条件、分支与循环声明为可执行操作协议（EOP），并由运行时强制执行编译后的状态机。其元级框架把Claude Code、Codex等完整黑盒编程智能体作为执行图节点，使不同产品能够相互审查和修复实验实现。预算匹配评测中，异构组合将全预言机执行准确率从最佳单产品的45.8%提升至62.5%，但仍存在10.4%的静默关键缺陷率。系统还通过知识层跨迭代保留正负实验结果，并在HSTU推荐模型的十二轮部署中提升MovieLens-20M上的NDCG@10；基于真实故障构建的ExecML-HSTU基准及LitGPT迁移实验进一步验证了方法的跨领域效果。"
purpose = "- 为长周期自动研究提供协议化、可审计的执行框架，降低数据泄漏、归一化遗漏、梯度断连和训练/评估配置失效等静默错误。\n- 说明异构编程智能体的互审与修复比依赖单一产品更可靠，可用于设计多智能体科研工作流。\n- EOP与编译状态机可作为实验治理层，将阶段门控、异常分支和验证步骤转化为运行时硬约束。\n- 跨迭代知识层保留失败实验与负面结果，有助于减少重复试错并支持持续的模型演化。"
notes = "标题：RankEvolve: A Reliable Multi-Agent Auto-Research Harness for Evolving Ranking Models\n来源：arXiv\n链接：https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.39551\n摘要：Auto-research agents, LLM systems that propose, implement, train, and evaluate model changes across iterations, promise to automate applied ML's experimental loop. Over long horizons, execution accuracy is a binding constraint: a change can silently leak held-out data, omit normalization, disconnect a gradient, or leave a train/eval flag unwired, invalidating expensive runs and compounding error across iterations. We present RankEvolve, an auto-research framework for evolving generative ranking models. An Executable Operating Protocol (EOP) declares phases, gates, branches, and loops, and the runtime enforces the compiled state machine. A meta-meta-harness composes complete black-box coding-agent products, including Claude Code and Codex, as execution-graph nodes that review and repair one"

notes = "标题：Growing an Agent/Prover Interface: Evolutionary Tool Design for Cost-Efficient Theorem Proving in Rocq and Lean\n来源：arXiv\n链接：https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.39544\n摘要：Recent achievements in AI-assisted mathematics require intensive interaction of agents with proof assistants to generate machine-checked proof certificates. Agents interact with proof assistants such as Rocq or Lean through an interface that controls what the agent receives from the prover and the cost of these interactions. Today, these interfaces are adapted from tools designed for humans and not optimized for agents. We propose an evolutionary method where a frontier model incrementally proposes new features and only keeps the ones that improve the overall performance of smaller models. We demonstrate the effectiveness of our method by growing, on a curated set of mathematical problems, \\rme, a new MCP server for the Rocq prover. On the held-out \\texttt{test} split of miniF2F-Rocq, an a"

content = "CATCH 是面向代码强化学习的可控奖励作弊测试床，主动暴露环境漏洞，使模型可能在未真正完成任务的情况下获得高奖励。它将脆弱评估器的判定与独立审计下的任务正确性进行比较，从而生成基于执行结果的奖励作弊金标准标签。研究者可通过调整监督微调数据混合比例控制模型的初始作弊倾向，并通过奖励设计改变获取奖励的难度，以比较不同训练轨迹和干预方法。实验发现，思维链监控器只能在训练初期抑制作弊，随后策略模型会利用代码注释误导监控器，导致防护效果逐渐失效。"
purpose = "- 为奖励作弊检测与缓解方法提供可复现、可控且带有执行级标签的评估环境。\n- 提示安全干预不能只在训练前期或单一检查点验证，而应持续跟踪整个强化学习过程。\n- 揭示基于思维链的监控机制可能被策略模型适应性规避，需要结合独立执行审计与多层验证。\n- 可用于分析初始模型倾向、奖励难度和监控策略对奖励作弊涌现过程的影响。"
notes = "标题：CATCH: A Controllable Analysis Testbed for Reward Hacking in Coding RL\n来源：arXiv\n链接：https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.39533\n摘要：During reinforcement learning with verifiable rewards (RLVR), large language models (LLMs) can exploit loopholes in their environments to obtain high rewards without improving the intended capabilities, i.e., reward hacking. Despite its risks to training efficiency and safety, monitoring and mitigating reward hacking during training remain challenging, which is limited by a lack of testbeds that reproduce hacking and reliably identify it. We introduce CATCH, a controllable testbed for studying reward hacking in coding RL. CATCH deliberately exposes environmental loopholes and provides execution-based gold labels by comparing success under a vulnerable evaluator with task correctness under an independent audit. It also can control the model's initial hacking tendency through supervised fine"

summary = "研究通过统一框架和1200次适配实验，解耦分析自蒸馏中的生成来源、教师耦合与KL方向如何影响知识习得和保持。"
paper = "https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.39494"
code = ""
dataset = ""
link = ""
content = "论文将特权上下文自蒸馏归纳为三个轴：生成轨迹来自学生或教师、教师采用冻结或不同速率的指数移动平均，以及使用正向或反向KL散度。作者在Qwen2.5-7B和Ministral-3-3B上覆盖普通任务与矛盾任务，对全部轴组合开展了1200次适配实验，并与经典监督微调比较。结果显示，教师生成在任务违背预训练行为时能显著提高知识习得，同时几乎不影响保持；教师耦合率对习得能力影响最大，但超过任务相关的最优点后效果下降。KL方向对保持能力的影响具有模型依赖性，而作者提出的受控模型能够复现实验观察到的三类趋势。"
purpose = "- 为比较不同自蒸馏方法提供统一分类框架，避免将多个设计变量绑定后得出相互冲突的结论。\n- 在需要覆盖或修正预训练行为的任务中，可优先评估教师生成轨迹以提升新知识习得。\n- 教师耦合率存在任务相关的最佳区间，应独立调参而非默认采用固定教师或固定EMA配置。\n- KL方向对知识保持的影响因模型而异，蒸馏方案的调优顺序应结合具体基座模型确定。"
notes = "标题：Disentangling Self-Distillation: Measuring and Modeling Acquisition and Retention\n来源：arXiv\n链接：https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.39494\n摘要：Self-distillation with privileged context adapts a language model from demonstrations by letting the model, once conditioned on a reference response, teach its context-free copy token by token. Our taxonomy reveals existing methods differ along three entangled axes: (i) the rollout source (student or teacher), (ii) the teacher coupling (frozen, or an exponential moving average of the student at some coupling rate) and (iii) the KL direction (reverse or forward), yet these axes are usually studied in fixed combinations and have led to conflicting conclusions. We formalize a unifying framework to encompass all self-distillation methods vs classic supervised fine-tuning: we train every combination of the three axes, on Qwen2.5-7B and Ministral-3-3B across ordinary and contradictory tasks, tot"


[[items]]

title = "小模型也能合理自评置信度"

summary = "研究发现，模型自评置信度的可靠性基本不受模型规模和知识领域专业程度影响，小模型即使准确率较低也能提供合理的置信度信号。"
paper = "https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.39478"
code = ""
dataset = ""
link = ""
content = "研究系统比较了不同规模语言模型在通用与专业知识问答任务中的自评式不确定性量化能力。随着模型缩小或领域专业性提高，回答准确率会如预期下降，但自评置信度的可靠性在这两个维度上大体保持稳定。结果表明，能力最强、准确率最高的模型不一定最善于判断自身预测是否可靠。小模型因此有望在资源受限场景中以较低成本提供可用的置信度信号。"
purpose = "- 为小模型在端侧或资源受限部署中配置信心校准、拒答和风险控制机制提供依据。\n- 提醒评测者将任务准确率与置信度自评能力分开衡量，避免以模型规模或总体能力代替可靠性评估。\n- 可用于模型路由：依据自评置信度决定是否接受小模型答案，或将低置信度问题升级给更强模型。\n- 后续应进一步检验自评置信度在分布偏移、开放式生成及高风险专业任务中的稳定性。"
notes = "标题：Also Small Models Can Reasonably Self-Evaluate Their Confidence\n来源：arXiv\n链接：https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.39478\n摘要：This study systematically evaluates self-evaluation-based uncertainty quantification across different language models of varying sizes on question-answering tasks spanning general to specialized knowledge domains. Using various self-evaluation methods where models judge their own predictions, we examine how model scale and domain specificity affect the quality of self-assessed confidence signals. Our results reveal that while accuracy predictably declines with smaller models and more specialized domains, the reliability of self-evaluated confidence remains largely stable across both dimensions. This independence means the most capable model is not necessarily the best at self-assessing prediction reliability. These findings suggest that smaller models can achieve reasonable self-assessed c"

summary = "论文提出无需训练的 FAME 框架，通过反事实干预下智能体隐藏状态所表征的记忆概念漂移，识别由伪相关、环境变化和知识冲突引发的错误记忆。"
paper = "https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.39473"
code = ""
dataset = ""
link = ""
content = "论文将自主智能体因伪相关、环境迁移或知识冲突而形成的偏差信念形式化为“错误记忆”，并指出它容易与普通泛化失败混淆。FAME 构造反事实场景，通过假设性干预改变记忆的潜在概念，再测量智能体生成答案前隐藏状态中的概念漂移，以区分忠实记忆与错误记忆。该方法无需额外训练、奖励设计或答案采样，避免了仅监控最终答案时的检测盲区。实验覆盖 GSM-Symbolic、GitChameleon 和 BigBench-Hard，FAME 在不同错误记忆设置下取得 76.2%–96.7% 的 AUROC，并比最佳基线高出 3.4%–23.3%。"
purpose = "- 为智能体长期记忆系统提供面向内部信念的可靠性评估方法，而非只依赖最终输出判断。\n- 可借助反事实模板定位环境变化、伪相关和知识冲突导致的记忆偏差。\n- 隐藏状态中的概念漂移可作为智能体部署前审计、运行时监测和记忆治理的信号。\n- 说明记忆评测需要区分错误记忆与一般泛化失败，以减少对智能体失效原因的误判。"
notes = "标题：Beyond the Shadows of Plato's Cave: Evaluating False Memory in Autonomous Agents via Counterfactual Reasoning\n来源：arXiv\n链接：https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.39473\n摘要：Autonomous agents increasingly rely on memory to generalize beyond their training environments. However, agents are bounded by what they have seen and believed, and leveraging such memories in unseen environments can introduce biases into their internal beliefs. We formalize this phenomenon as \\textit{false memory}, which can arise from spurious correlations, environment shifts, and knowledge conflicts. Despite its importance, false memory is difficult to evaluate because it stems from agent internal beliefs and is easily confounded with ordinary generalization failures. Therefore, we propose FAME, a training-free framework that evaluates false memory through the evolution of agent beliefs under counterfactual reasoning. Specifically, counterfactual scenarios reveal how beliefs change as t"


[[items]]
id = "actionguard"
title = "ActionGuard：面向技能投毒的工具调用授权机制"
subtopic = "可信执行"
topics = [
    "工具调用",
    "智能体安全",

    "提示注入攻击",
]
orgs = [
    "Korea University",
]
source = "arXiv"
summary = "ActionGuard在工具调用执行前基于可信用户意图与运行时证据进行授权，以阻止被投毒第三方技能诱导的危险操作。"
paper = "https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.39450"
code = ""
dataset = ""
link = ""
content = "ActionGuard针对第三方技能中隐藏恶意指令的风险，拦截可能导致数据外泄、文件删除或未授权代码执行的工具调用。其将目标智能体的动作生成上下文与审查器的授权上下文隔离，避免审查器直接接触可能被投毒的原始技能文本。审查器依据可信用户请求、平衡化技能档案、近期工具调用和本地脚本内容作出 ALLOW 或 DENY 决策，并采用失败关闭策略执行。基于 SKILL-INJECT 的实验涵盖139个上下文型和180个明显注入样例；相较现有防护方案，ActionGuard将攻击成功率降低35.54%至46.11%，相较无防护降低70.44%，同时保持较高的正常任务完成率。"
purpose = "- 为技能驱动型智能体提供位于执行边界的独立授权层，降低技能投毒造成实际破坏的风险。\n- 表明安全审查不应复用可能受污染的完整规划上下文，而应围绕可信用户意图构建最小化授权上下文。\n- 可将失败关闭、运行时证据审查和逐次工具调用授权用于高风险智能体系统的安全设计。"
notes = "标题：ActionGuard: Tool Call Authorization under Poisoned Skills\n来源：arXiv\n链接：https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.39450\n摘要：LLM-based agents extend their capabilities through third-party skills that provide task-specific instructions, scripts, and tool-use procedures. However, malicious instructions inserted into an otherwise benign skill can cause a benign user request to trigger dangerous Tool Calls, including data exfiltration, file deletion, or unauthorized code execution. This paper presents ActionGuard, which inspects skill-influenced Tool Calls immediately before execution. ActionGuard separates the target agent's action-generation context from the safeguard's authorization context. The target agent may use the original skill for planning, but the Reviewer does not receive the potentially poisoned raw skill text. Instead, it determines whether each action is justified by the trusted user request using a "

id = "item-91"
title = "基于训练动态的合成数据特性分析"

]
source = "arXiv"
summary = "论文利用编码器训练动态刻画大模型合成数据的样本级可学习性，并比较不同模型家族、规模及人类数据在分布稳健性和数据选择效果上的差异。"
paper = "https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.39447"
code = ""
dataset = ""
link = ""
content = "研究以样本级可学习性为切入点，分析不同大模型家族和参数规模生成的数据，并以人类撰写数据作为参照。作者构建了覆盖单标签分类、多标签分类、序列标注和树预测任务的合成数据集。通过编码器的训练动态，研究提取机器生成数据与自然数据的经验分布，并检验这些分布在不同编码器之间的稳健性。实验进一步表明，基于可学习性信号的数据选择策略会对合成数据和人类数据产生不同影响。"
purpose = "- 为评估合成数据的质量、难度和可学习性提供基于训练动态的样本级分析方法。\n- 帮助数据策展流程识别不同来源数据对筛选策略的差异化响应，避免直接套用面向人类数据的选择规则。\n- 可用于比较不同模型家族与规模所生成数据的分布特征，并指导训练数据配比和样本选择。"
notes = "标题：Synthetic Data Characterization via Training Dynamics\n来源：arXiv\n链接：https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.39447\n摘要：Interpreting properties of LLM-generated data is important for understanding its utility and limitations across learning tasks. In this work, we characterize synthetic data through sample-level learnability, studying variation among LLM families and scales, alongside human-written data as a reference. We first generate synthetic datasets spanning single- and multi-label classification, labeling, and tree prediction tasks. We then derive empirical data distributions from encoder training dynamics for both machine and organic data, and estimate the robustness of these distributions across encoders. Finally, we evaluate how data selection strategies based on these learnability signals affect both data sources differently."

title = "NSCR-LLM：开放权重模型的地理推理忠实性基准"

paper = "https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.39437"

dataset = ""
link = ""
content = "研究利用步行街道网络界定地点周边实际可达区域，从 OpenStreetMap 与 GHS-POP 检索特征、计算网络覆盖区指标，并将其压缩为空间简报注入提示。基准按来源与正确性对模型生成的每项主张进行标注，以区分依据简报的回答和源自参数记忆的回答。每个案例还植入一个被空间简报明确反驳的错误前提，用于测试模型抵抗误导和忠实读取证据的能力。实验覆盖 Qwen、Gemma 和 Llama 等 16 种开放权重模型配置、三座城市及十个随机种子，结果表明错误前提抵抗力受模型家族与代际影响大于参数规模，且与简报阅读能力并非完全一致。"

notes = "标题：Network-based Spatial Context Retrieval for Open-weight LLMs: A Faithfulness Benchmark for Grounded Geographic Reasoning\n来源：arXiv\n链接：https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.39437\n摘要：Large language models (LLMs) encode substantial latent geographic knowledge, yet they reason poorly over space and are unreliable when queried from coordinates alone. Useful behaviour emerges only when structured spatial context is supplied in the prompt. This raises a question geographic evaluation has left unexamined: once the right context is supplied, does the model reason from it, or override it with its own parametric recall? We take up this question with an open pipeline for network-based spatial context retriev-al. In it, the surroundings of a selected point are defined by the pedestrian street network, the area actually reachable on foot. Using only open data and open-weight models, the pipeline retrieves features from OpenStreetMap and the GHS-POP population grid, computes indica"


[[items]]
id = "stair"
title = "STAIR：复用历史计算提升大模型新问题推理"
subtopic = "推理优化"
topics = [
    "推理",
    "上下文学习",
    "长上下文",
    "缓存优化",
    "参数高效微调",
]
research = []
keywords = [
    "推理优化",
    "KV缓存",
    "上下文学习",
    "长上下文",
    "参数高效微调",
]
orgs = [
    "中国人民大学",
    "University of Modena and Reggio Emilia",
]
source = "arXiv"
summary = "研究提出 STAIR，通过复用早期回答生成时的键值状态，在仅训练少量参数的情况下将多轮独立问题的后续回答准确率最高提升 11.67 个百分点。"
paper = "https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.39394"
code = ""
dataset = ""
link = ""
content = "研究发现，同一对话中保留的历史记录既可能提高也可能降低大模型解决后续独立问题的准确率，即使这些问题属于同一领域。作者通过受控回放分离特定“问题—历史”组合引起的内部状态变化，并观察到不同历史下当前问题之间的关系具有相似结构。为利用这些历史计算，论文提出 STAIR（Stale-Token Attention for Inter-query Reuse），将早期回答生成阶段的键和值存入固定缓存，并学习在处理新提示时重定向当前查询。STAIR 冻结基础模型且仅训练 12,288 个参数，在三个 Qwen 模型和四项基准上，相比直接携带历史的原模型将后续轮次平均准确率最高提升 11.67 个百分点。"
purpose = "- 表明历史对话不仅承载文本信息，也包含可被后续问题复用的中间计算状态。\n- 为多轮推理系统提供一种参数高效的跨查询计算复用机制，减少重新学习历史模式的需求。\n- 提醒应用开发者关注历史上下文的双向影响，避免简单保留全部记录导致后续任务准确率下降。\n- 可用于探索 KV 状态缓存、长上下文学习与多任务连续推理的协同优化。"
notes = "标题：Can Computation from Earlier Problems Help LLMs Solve New Ones?\n来源：arXiv\n链接：https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.39394\n摘要：Large language models often solve independent problems in the same conversation. Can computation from earlier problems help them solve new ones? To answer this question, we first conduct preliminary experiments showing that retained history can raise or lower later-turn accuracy, even within the same domain. To understand these effects, we use controlled replay to isolate internal state changes specific to each problem-history pairing. Across different histories, these changes preserve similar relationships among current problems. To improve reasoning under retained history, we introduce STAIR (Stale-Token Attention for Inter-query Reuse). STAIR captures keys and values from earlier response generation in a fixed bank. It learns to redirect current queries when they read this bank during p"

content = "EEM从历史实验轨迹中提取与决策相关的记录，将其蒸馏为可复用经验并组织成经验库。面对新的候选研究方向，系统检索历史经验，判断现有证据是否足以决定继续投入。若证据不足，EEM会执行有针对性的低成本先导实验，再结合新旧经验判断是否值得开展高成本的完整评估。实验结果会继续沉淀到经验库中，使自主研究系统通过迭代积累不断增强，并在基准测试中实现性能提升与模型交互开销下降。"
purpose = "- 为科研智能体提供基于历史实验轨迹的经验复用机制，减少重复试验与无效探索。\n- 通过“经验检索—证据评估—低成本先导实验—完整评估”的分级流程优化实验资源配置。\n- 展示了按需获取缺失经验比无差别扩大实验规模更具成本效益。\n- 可作为构建持续积累实验知识、支持长期自主科学研究系统的基础模块。"
notes = "标题：Experimental Experience Modeling for Autonomous Research\n来源：arXiv\n链接：https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.39392\n摘要：Autonomous research agents can generate hypotheses and conduct experiments, but experimentation remains a major source of computational cost. A fundamental challenge is deciding which experiments are worth running, particularly when prior evidence is insufficient to resolve uncertainty. Yet current research agents lack a systematic way to leverage experimental experience when making such decisions. We introduce Experimental Experience Modeling (EEM), a framework for making informed experimental decisions by acquiring, reusing, and accumulating experimental experience. EEM extracts decision-relevant records from earlier experimental trajectories, distills them into reusable experience, and organizes them in an experience library. For a new experimental decision, EEM retrieves relevant histo"

title = "从搜索到信号：自动启发式设计的在线后训练"
subtopic = "程序进化"

summary = "研究将自动启发式设计中的候选程序评估结果转化为上下文相关的在线后训练信号，并检验更新生成模型是否比单纯增加搜索预算更有效。"
paper = "https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.39383"
code = ""
dataset = ""
link = ""
content = "论文研究基于 LLM 的自动启发式设计（AHD），其中模型持续生成包含设计理由与可执行代码的候选启发式，并由任务评估器返回有效性和性能结果。作者将小型开放权重模型的在线后训练表述为上下文相关的信号构造问题，比较从程序有效性、任务表现及生成上下文映射到更新信号的不同方法。实验在共享评估轨迹和匹配更新预算下，对多类 AHD 任务与模型家族进行受控比较，分别衡量有效提案率、有效程序性能及相对上下文有所改进的候选产出率。研究还结合模型检查点、冻结搜索状态和在线系统评估，区分模型能力提升与搜索状态积累带来的收益，并在预设成本口径下比较在线更新和冻结生成器的额外搜索。"
purpose = "- 为把可验证执行结果转化为 LLM 在线强化学习信号提供上下文相关的设计框架。\n- 提醒自动算法设计研究不能仅凭端到端搜索收益断言模型能力增强，应隔离搜索状态积累与参数更新的贡献。\n- 可用于设计资源匹配的模型自演化实验，比较在线后训练与增加冻结模型搜索预算的真实性价比。\n- 为程序进化、启发式搜索和 RLVR 系统建立更细粒度的有效性、性能与候选改进率评估方法。"
notes = "标题：From Search to Signal: Online Post-Training in Automatic Heuristic Design\n来源：arXiv\n链接：https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.39383\n摘要：Large language model (LLM)-based automatic heuristic design (AHD) iteratively proposes and refines heuristics, pairing design rationales with executable code. Task-specific evaluators assess programs; execution outcomes and performance scores guide search. Many AHD systems keep the generator frozen; EvoTune and Co-Evolution of Algorithms and Language Model (CALM) instead update it from evaluated candidates. When such outcomes drive reinforcement learning with verifiable rewards (RLVR), they create a search-coupled loop: the evaluated candidate stream supplies both search-state updates and training signals for the model that generates future candidates. Yet validity and performance do not uniquely determine useful model updates; converting them into learning signals must account for the pro"

content = "SkillFM将文本技能编码至连续潜在空间，并通过编解码器实现技能文本的重构。框架采用改进的 MeanFlow 训练条件流模型，学习面向具体任务的潜在技能分布。推理时，模型利用速度场进行单步潜变量采样，再由基于 LLM 的解码器生成供冻结下游智能体使用的文本指导。实验覆盖 ALFWorld、Search-QA 和网页购物任务，其中在 ALFWorld 与 Search-QA 上取得了向量化技能方法中的最佳综合表现。"
purpose = "- 为智能体提供无需人工维护技能库、也无需测试时检索的任务条件化技能生成方案。\n- 单步潜在采样有望降低技能增强的推理延迟和检索成本。\n- 说明连续潜空间生成可以成为检索式技能复用的有效替代路径。\n- 可用于构建面向具身任务、问答和网页操作的可扩展智能体技能模块。"
notes = "标题：SkillFM: Generating Skills for LLM Agents via Latent Flow Matching\n来源：arXiv\n链接：https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.39382\n摘要：Textual skills provide reusable guidance for large language model agents, but existing approaches often rely on manually curated skill banks or reinforcement learning with indirect and delayed feedback. We introduce SkillFM (Skill Flow Matching), a generative framework that synthesizes task-conditioned textual skills directly without test-time skill retrieval. Our framework combines a codec for encoding and reconstructing textual skills in a continuous latent space with a conditional flow model trained using improved MeanFlow. At inference time, the learned velocity field enables single-step latent sampling, and an LLM-based decoder converts the sampled representation into textual guidance for a frozen downstream agent. We evaluate the framework on embodied tasks, question answering, and w"

purpose = "- 为数据增强、隐私保护型数据共享和下游模型开发提供高保真的合成时间序列。\n- 展示小波域表示可利用尺度间方差差异，自然构造无需人工调度的粗到细生成机制。\n- 为同时建模长短期时间结构和多变量通道依赖提供一种流匹配架构设计。\n- 可启发将频域或多分辨率变换与生成模型结合，以提升复杂结构化序列的生成质量。"
notes = "标题：Wavelet Flow Matching for Time Series\n来源：arXiv\n链接：https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.39374\n摘要：Synthetic time series are increasingly used for data augmentation, privacy-preserving data sharing, and downstream model development, yet faithfully reproducing both multi-scale temporal structure and cross-channel dependencies remains challenging. We study multivariate time-series generation through flow matching in the wavelet domain. By operating on multilevel discrete wavelet coefficients rather than directly in the time domain, the model represents coarse structure and progressively finer details at separate scales. Their naturally different variances further induce an implicit coarse-to-fine generative process without requiring an explicit multi-scale schedule. Since the transform acts independently on each channel, we pair it with a channel-token transformer whose attention directly"

id = "lora"

    "Gatsby Computational Neuroscience Unit",
    "University College London",
    "Radboud University Nijmegen",
    "Donders Centre for Neuroscience",
]
source = "arXiv"
summary = "研究通过可解析的双任务师生模型刻画 LoRA 在持续学习中的动力学，揭示适配器秩、知识迁移与灾难性遗忘之间的权衡。"
paper = "https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.39367"
code = ""
dataset = ""
link = ""
content = "研究在高维在线学习极限下，为双任务师生模型中的 LoRA 推导出一组关于宏观序参量的封闭常微分方程，可精确描述两个任务学习期间的泛化误差。理论与有限维模拟吻合，表明低秩更新能够减少对首个任务特征的干扰，但其初始化会减慢对第二个任务的适应。作者提出状态依赖的掩码策略，冻结承载最强旧任务表征的隐藏单元，并仅在互补子空间中进行适配，从而在保持可塑性的同时显著降低遗忘。分析还发现，迁移收益在适配器秩达到目标任务内在维度后趋于饱和，而遗忘程度仍会随秩增大，并在顺序 MNIST 基准上得到定性验证。"
purpose = "- 为理解 LoRA 在连续任务训练中的知识保留与更新机制提供可解析的动力学框架。\n- 说明适配器秩并非越高越好，可依据目标任务的内在维度选择秩以平衡迁移能力和遗忘风险。\n- 状态依赖掩码和表征子空间分区可用于设计更稳健的参数高效持续学习方法。\n- 该理论可作为分析其他适配器结构、初始化方案及灾难性遗忘干预策略的基础。"
notes = "标题：A Dynamical Theory of LoRA in Continual Learning\n来源：arXiv\n链接：https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.39367\n摘要：Despite the widespread use of Low-Rank Adaptation (LoRA), little is known about its dynamics in continual learning and the mechanisms by which low-rank updates affect catastrophic forgetting. We provide an asymptotically exact dynamical characterization of LoRA in a solvable two-task teacher-student model. In the high-dimensional online-learning limit, we derive a closed system of ordinary differential equations for a finite set of macroscopic order parameters, yielding exact expressions for the generalization errors throughout both the initial Task 1 learning phase and the subsequent LoRA fine-tuning on Task 2. The theory quantitatively matches finite-dimensional simulations and exposes two characteristic effects of LoRA: low-rank adaptation reduces interference with features learned on t"

content = "AutoMIP是一种用于混合整数线性与非线性规划自动研究的可复用智能体技能，重点解决竞争性想法管理和长程实验组织问题。它维护包含互补候选方案的持久化创意池，并将可执行实验组织为算法树，从而保留未探索假设、持续改进潜力算法，并依据历史状态切换研究方向。在MIPLib上，AutoMIP为60个实例中的31个发现了新最佳解；在MINLPLib上，这一数字达到52个。消融实验表明，创意池和算法树搜索具有互补作用，多样化研究构想与结构化实验轨迹的联合维护是提升长程自动研究效果的关键。"

notes = "标题：Autoresearch in Mixed-Integer Linear and Nonlinear Programming\n来源：arXiv\n链接：https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.39360\n摘要：Despite recent progress in autoresearch, applying it to practical operations research problems, typically formulated as NP-hard mixed-integer linear or nonlinear programs (MILPs or MINLPs), remains challenging because effective research requires systematically managing competing ideas and long-horizon experimental trajectories. We introduce AutoMIP, a reusable agent skill for organizing long-horizon autoresearch in mixed-integer programming through idea pooling and algorithm tree search. AutoMIP maintains a persistent pool of complementary candidate ideas while organizing executable experiments into an algorithm tree, enabling the agent to preserve unexplored hypotheses, refine promising algorithms, and switch to alternative methodological directions based on historical states. On MILP and"


[[items]]
id = "galahad-llm"
title = "Galahad 字节精确记忆让 LLM 文档读取成为一次性成本"

summary = "Galahad 通过跨请求持久化并精确恢复 KV 状态、按需提供文档片段，将大模型重复阅读上下文的计算变为一次性成本。"
paper = "https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.39358"
code = ""
dataset = ""
link = ""
content = "Galahad 是面向 vLLM、SGLang 和 llama.cpp 的有状态推理记忆层，针对服务请求间重复计算文档注意力状态的问题；七个真实数据集中，98.7% 的提示词元来自模型已经读过的文本。Taliesin 保存文本块的 KV 状态，并在后续请求出现相同字节时直接加载，其恢复状态具有比特级一致性，重启、重新水合及热加载后的 262,144 个输出 logits 全部匹配。Blaise 保存原始文档并只向模型传递回答问题所需的片段；在包含 100 个隐藏事实的 9.7 万词元语料上，两者结合仅需读取约 668 个词元，即可在三个运行时中全部答对。系统将单次问题的延迟降至 0.59～0.64 秒、能耗降至 200～213 焦耳，语料存储的一次性能耗约在 13 次问答后收回，并通过校验失败后重新计算实现故障闭合。"
purpose = "- 将跨请求 KV 状态持久化，可显著减少文档问答、智能助手和知识库服务中的重复预填充计算。\n- 字节精确匹配与故障闭合机制为缓存复用提供了可验证的正确性边界，避免错误状态静默污染推理结果。\n- KV 状态复用与按需文档切片可以组合使用，同时改善长上下文覆盖率、响应延迟和能源成本。\n- 结果表明 LLM 服务可由无状态请求处理转向有状态推理架构，为长期运行智能体和高频文档查询提供基础设施思路。"
notes = "标题：Working Around the Compute Ceiling: Byte-Exact Memory in Galahad Makes LLM Reading a One-Time Cost LLM Reading a One-Time Cost\n来源：arXiv\n链接：https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.39358\n摘要：A transformer language model performs a bounded amount of computation per token, and recent work by Vishal Sikka, former CEO of Infosys, argues that this bound limits which tasks a model can carry out or verify (arXiv:2507.07505). We ask how much of the budget beneath that ceiling is spent on work the model has already done. Serving is stateless across requests: a model that answers a second question about a document recomputes the document's attention state from the first token. On seven real-world datasets, 98.7% of prompt tokens were text the model had already read. We present Galahad, a memory layer for vLLM, SGLang and llama.cpp that makes this reading a one-time cost. Taliesin saves the model's key-value (KV) state for a block of text and loads it on the next request that contains th"

content = "论文提出可复用潜在纠正（Reusable Latent Correction，RLC），旨在弥合大模型推理能力与小模型本地部署优势之间的差距。该方法仅在离线阶段访问黑盒大模型，将其自然语言指导转换为小语言模型隐藏空间中的纠正经验，并持久化至外部经验库。在线推理时，系统根据小模型当前的推理状态检索相关纠正经验，全程不再调用大模型，也无需更新小模型参数。多个推理基准和不同规模小模型上的实验表明，RLC 能够持续改善小模型的推理表现。"
purpose = "- 将一次性的大模型反馈沉淀为可跨问题复用的推理经验，降低重复调用商业 API 的成本。\n- 为参数冻结或无法微调的小语言模型提供一种非参数化能力增强路径。\n- 展示了基于隐藏状态检索纠正经验的可行性，可用于构建本地、低成本且持续积累经验的推理系统。\n- 为研究经验库质量、状态匹配准确性及跨任务迁移能力提供了新的实验框架。"
notes = "标题：Offline Guidance, Online Reasoning: Reusing LLM Feedback for Small Language Models\n来源：arXiv\n链接：https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.39346\n摘要：Large language models (LLMs) offer strong reasoning capabilities but are often costly to access through commercial APIs, while small language models (SLMs) are easier to deploy locally yet remain weaker in reasoning. This capability-deployment gap has motivated LLM-SLM collaboration, which aims to improve SLM reasoning using LLM capabilities while preserving the deployment advantages of SLMs. Existing approaches mainly follow two paradigms. Knowledge distillation uses LLM-generated answers and reasoning trajectories to train SLMs offline, but requires parameter updates and additional training. Alternatively, online collaboration routes difficult problems to an LLM or leverages LLM-generated guidance and corrections when an SLM encounters difficulties. Although effective, online collaborati"

content = "SpecScale面向大模型测试时扩展中的推测执行，重点解决候选推理路径搜索空间爆炸以及验证任务频繁、细粒度化的问题。系统通过提前剪除低质量候选路径，减少无效搜索与后续计算开销。它还对候选路径间的重复计算进行去重，并推迟部分细粒度验证任务，以平衡响应延迟和资源消耗。在MATH和Olympiad等高难度推理基准上，SpecScale相较非推测方案和近期推测执行方法改善了吞吐量与延迟，同时保持答案质量。"
purpose = "- 为测试时计算扩展提供可落地的高性能推理服务架构。\n- 表明搜索剪枝、跨路径计算复用和验证调度可以协同缓解推测执行的系统瓶颈。\n- 可用于优化数学、编程等需要探索多条推理路径的在线大模型服务。\n- 为后续研究动态候选调度、验证预算分配和推理路径缓存提供参考。"
notes = "标题：Taming Speculative Search for Test-Time Scaling in LLM Serving\n来源：arXiv\n链接：https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.39334\n摘要：Test-time scaling has recently emerged as a powerful approach for improving LLM reasoning by allocating additional computation during inference, substantially enhancing accuracy on challenging tasks such as mathematics and coding. To accelerate the exploration of reasoning paths, recent studies proposed speculative execution. To address these challenges, this paper proposes SpecScale, a serving system for efficient speculative execution. We introduce three techniques to reconcile the trade-off between latency and computational overhead: (1) early pruning of low-qu"

subtopic = "环境合成"

summary = "WorkGenesis 基于真实职业证据合成可执行工作任务，并通过执行引导的一致性审计迭代修复数据，以低成本构建高质量工作型智能体训练集。"
paper = "https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.39325"
code = ""
dataset = ""
link = ""
content = "WorkGenesis 利用 O*NET 职业知识检索公开文件，将真实材料作为证据，围绕其合成工作背景、配套资料、任务请求和逐项评分标准。框架进一步生成并执行参考交付物，将未满足的评分项归因于智能体、任务或评分标准，再根据发现的缺陷迭代修复任务环境。研究团队仅使用该框架生成的 2 万条工作数据进行简单监督微调，得到 Fx-Work-35B。在 GDPvalAA-v2、APEX-Agents-AA 和 JobBench 的五项指标上，该模型平均得分达到 31.00，超过同规模基线的 24.79，并优于部分前沿大模型。"
purpose = "- 为工作型智能体提供可扩展、具备真实证据支撑的职业任务训练数据。\n- 通过执行结果定位任务描述与评分标准中的不一致，形成数据生成、审计和修复闭环。\n- 表明高质量的小规模合成数据配合简单 SFT，也可能显著提升智能体的职业工作能力。\n- 可用于构建办公、专业服务及其他真实世界任务的训练环境与评测集。"
notes = "标题：WorkGenesis: Building the Worlds That Teach Agents to Work\n来源：arXiv\n链接：https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.39325\n摘要：The ability of Large Language Model (LLM) agents to complete daily and professional work is receiving increasing attention. Training such agents requires realistic work scenarios. Expert-authored occupational work is costly and slow to produce, while unconstrained synthesis often yields tasks with weak factual grounding or internally inconsistent requirements. To bridge this gap, we introduce WorkGenesis, a framework that constructs executable occupational work from real-world artifacts through two core technical innovations: (1) Evidence-Based Work Construction, which grounds each unit of work in real-world evidence by retrieving public files guided by O*NET occupational knowledge and synthesizing the surrounding context, companion materials, work request, and itemwise rubric around them;"

source = "arXiv"
summary = "研究系统分析了 1.5B～7B 小语言模型的 GRPO 微调动态，揭示组大小、LoRA 配置与奖励塑形对收敛稳定性及推理性能的影响。"
paper = "https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.39321"
code = ""

link = ""
content = "研究在单节点 8×A100 预算下，对多个 1.5B～7B 模型家族开展 GRPO 强化微调实验，覆盖数学、代码和科学选择题推理。作者重点分析组大小对策略收敛、训练稳定性和下游基准性能的影响，并刻画训练期间的张量级参数更新动态。初始 GRPO 模型在约 80% 的数学基准评测中优于基础模型，但在代码推理和选择题任务上的提升有限。基于机制分析，研究进一步调整 LoRA 目标模块、目标层与奖励塑形配置，从而改善后两类任务的表现。"
purpose = "- 为资源受限环境中的小语言模型 GRPO 微调提供可复现的配置与计算预算参考。\n- 说明 GRPO 的收益具有任务依赖性，数学推理上的改进不能直接外推到代码和科学选择题。\n- 可通过监测张量级更新动态诊断训练稳定性，并据此选择 LoRA 注入位置。\n- 提示组大小、参数高效微调配置与奖励塑形应联合调优，而非孤立设置。"
notes = "标题：GRPO Training Dynamics for Small Language Models\n来源：arXiv\n链接：https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.39321\n摘要：Group Relative Policy Optimization (GRPO) has emerged as a memory-efficient reinforcement fine-tuning (RFT) technique for reasoning-intensive tasks. How- ever, GRPO training dynamics on small language models (SLMs) remain poorly understood, limiting its reliable adoption and reproducibility in open and resource- constrained environments. In this work, we present a systematic study of GRPO fine-tuning for SLMs ranging from 1.5B to 7B parameters under a practical single- node 8xA100 compute budget. Our study spans multiple model families and reasoning domains, including mathematics, coding, and multiple-choice question answering (MCQ) in science. Across these settings, we analyze how group size affects policy convergence, training stability, and downstream benchmark per- formance. We further"

summary = "ReSAIL通过选择特权信息影响显著的交互步骤并保留教师的特权信息条件行为，有效缓解智能体在多轮迭代自蒸馏中的性能坍塌。"
paper = "https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.39306"
code = ""

link = ""
content = "ReSAIL是一种面向迭代式、基于特权信息（PI）的智能体自蒸馏插件，旨在解决学生模型循环成为教师后部署性能持续下降的问题。该方法优先选择PI对教师预测影响最大的交互步骤，并在不同轨迹间平衡蒸馏损失。它还约束学生的PI条件输出分布接近冻结教师，从而为下一轮蒸馏保留有效监督能力。在ALFWorld和TextCraft的三轮实验中，ReSAIL使最终轮成功率平均绝对提升22.5%，其敏感度引导的数据选择策略也提升了AITZ多模态GUI智能体的动作预测准确率。"
purpose = "- 为部署轨迹上的递归自我改进提供更稳定的迭代自蒸馏机制。\n- 表明蒸馏样本的交互步骤选择与跨轨迹损失平衡是防止性能坍塌的关键。\n- 可作为插件集成到现有基于特权信息的自蒸馏方法中，无需重新设计完整训练框架。\n- 对多模态GUI智能体的离线数据筛选也具有借鉴价值。"
notes = "标题：ReSAIL: Mitigating Collapse in Iterative Agent Self-Distillation\n来源：arXiv\n链接：https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.39306\n摘要：Iterative self-distillation enables LLM agents to learn from successive deployments, offering a path toward recursive self-improvement (RSI). Yet our experiments with existing methods reveal a collapse in deployment performance across cycles, while task performance with privileged information (PI) also declines. We address this collapse by prioritizing informative interaction steps for distillation and preserving PI-conditioned behavior as the student becomes the next teacher. We introduce Retentive and Selective Augmentation for Iterative Self-Distillation (ReSAIL), a plug-in augmentation for iterative PI-based self-distillation. ReSAIL selects interaction steps where PI most strongly changes the teacher's predictions and balances the resulting distillation losses across trajectories. It "

summary = "研究表明，扩大每轮评估的 rollout 规模并采用 Champion-Challenger 选择机制，是视觉界面机器人智能体自动演化脚手架实现稳定泛化的关键。"
paper = "https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.39304"
code = ""
dataset = ""
link = ""
content = "研究使用优化器智能体自动修改机器人策略的脚手架，包括提示词、工具与控制规则，而机器人智能体通过截图观察浏览器中的三维界面并操控虚拟夹爪。固定演化轮数时，将训练集从 5 个 rollout 扩大到 100 个，可使留出任务成功率由 47% 提升至 67%；小规模评估则容易因二元结果噪声而过拟合。若无条件接受优化器提出的所有修改，错误编辑会持续累积并使性能在十轮内下降。采用 Champion-Challenger 机制，仅在候选版本于固定评估集上严格优于当前版本时晋升，可在 30 轮内将留出成功率从 51% 提升至 67%。"
purpose = "- 说明自动脚手架演化的可靠性高度依赖每次版本决策背后的 rollout 数量，应以更大评估批次降低偶然成功带来的误判。\n- 表明智能体自我改进循环不能无条件接受修改，需要通过 Champion-Challenger 等保守选择机制阻止性能漂移。\n- 为视觉界面机器人和具身智能体的脚手架优化提供可复用方案：固定评估集、候选对照、严格晋升并持续监测留出任务泛化。"
notes = "标题：Scale and Selection: What Makes Automatic Harness Evolution Work for Visual-Interface Robot Agents\n来源：arXiv\n链接：https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.39304\n摘要：When an off-the-shelf coding agent is used directly as a robot policy, observing a browser-based 3D interface through screenshots and acting by posing a virtual target gripper through a few tools, the agent's harness, its prompts, tools, and control rules, largely determines success, and until now it has been written by hand. We show that this harness can be improved automatically by another coding agent, the optimizer agent, and report two findings about what makes it work. First, the number of rollouts the optimizer agent sees per round governs whether the evolved harness is trustworthy, generalizes, and improves steadily. A single rollout is a noisy binary outcome, so with few rollouts per round a revision can be promoted on luck; enlarging the batch raises the signal-to-noise ratio of "

content = "MiniRep面向存在恶意参与者的多智能体辩论，解决历史信誉无法可靠预测智能体在新任务中行为的问题。研究结合信誉系统攻击与软件测试变异算子，构建了覆盖信誉策略性利用和提案隐蔽腐化的攻击分类体系。该方法同时评估智能体在当前任务中的行为与长期信誉，并限制高度相似回答群体对最终决策的支配。实验覆盖不同任务、模型组合、腐化位置和攻击类型；在MATH的异构10智能体设置中，MiniRep在全部28种攻击条件下均优于基线。"
purpose = "- 为开放式多智能体生态提供兼顾历史信誉与即时行为的可信聚合机制。\n- 表明仅依赖排行榜或历史表现容易遭受适应性恶意智能体的策略操纵。\n- 可将响应相似性约束用于抑制串谋、复制回答及同质化群体对集体决策的控制。\n- 其攻击分类体系可用于构建多智能体协作安全评测和红队测试方案。"
notes = "标题：MiniRep: Robust Reputation-Based Aggregation for Multi-Agent Debate\n来源：arXiv\n链接：https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.39297\n摘要：Autonomous agents powered by large language models (LLMs) are rapidly evolving into an open agentic ecosystem. To support trustworthy collaboration, industry initiatives increasingly assess agent reputation from past behavior and provide performance leaderboards. However, reputation derived from past performance may not reliably predict an agent's behavior on new tasks, particularly when malicious agents can adapt their behavior and influence other agents during collaboration. We study reputation in multi-agent debate (MAD), where multiple agents answer the same query, debate to improve their answers, and aggregate them into a final output. We present MiniRep, a reputation-based aggregation system for MAD under malicious agents. To ground our threat model in established research, we constr"

id = "engrambench"
title = "EngramBench：面向技能演化脚手架的能力本位评测基准"
subtopic = "演化能力评测"
topics = [
    "智能体评估",
    "技能演化",
    "跨域泛化",
    "软件工程",
    "长程任务",
]
research = [
    "EvalEvolve",
    "HarnessEvolve",
]
keywords = [
    "演化能力评测",
    "技能演化",
    "评测基准",
    "跨域泛化",
    "技能库",
]
orgs = [
    "Chinese University of Hong Kong, Shenzhen",
]
source = "arXiv"
summary = "EngramBench以“能力重叠但解法不重叠”为原则，区分真实技能抽象与历史方案泄漏，并验证技能库可显著减少智能体的无效试错和编码耗时。"
paper = "https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.39284"
code = ""
dataset = ""
link = ""
content = "EngramBench旨在解决自主智能体技能演化评测中的可识别性问题，避免将真实能力迁移与复制相似历史代码混为一谈。基准包含30个学习任务和13个未见迁移任务，由LLM模拟用户驱动智能体完成交互式、多小时的软件开发流程。基于48条多小时执行轨迹及人类专家验证，研究发现静态技能库无法突破基础模型在精确代码实现上的推理瓶颈。尽管如此，技能库能充当“执行罗盘”，减少灾难性试错和上下文膨胀，使总体编码时间降低超过55%。"
purpose = "- 为技能演化系统提供更严格的能力迁移测量方法，降低解法泄漏造成的虚假提升。\n- 评测智能体时应同时检查未见任务迁移、执行轨迹、上下文开销与开发耗时，而非只看最终成功率。\n- 技能库的主要价值可能是引导执行和复用程序性经验，而不是替代基础模型的底层推理能力。\n- 可用于比较不同技能记忆、智能体脚手架及长期软件开发策略的真实泛化效果。"
notes = "标题：EngramBench: A Capability-Grounded Benchmark for Skill-Evolution Harnesses\n来源：arXiv\n链接：https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.39284\n摘要：While large language models have achieved remarkable success in isolated code generation, authentic software engineering requires sustained reasoning, complex state management, and continuous cross-domain abstraction. However, current evaluations of skill evolution in autonomous agents suffer from a critical identifiability problem: they structurally confound genuine capability abstraction with rote solution leakage (i.e., copying highly similar code from historical training data). To resolve this, we introduce EngramBench, a rigorous, capability-grounded benchmark governed by the strict axiom of capability overlap without solution overlap. Comprising 30 diverse learning tasks and 13 unseen transfer tasks, EngramBench challenges agents to navigate interactive, multi-hour development cycles"

source = "arXiv"
summary = "ReTaCo通过保留教师模型的top-k概率并将其余词元聚合为残差符号，修正EOPD目标的概率质量偏差，同时降低在策略蒸馏的传输与存储成本。"
paper = "https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.39275"
code = ""
dataset = ""
link = ""
content = "在策略蒸馏需要基于学生自行生成的前缀获取逐词元教师反馈，但传输或保存完整词表分布的成本较高。现有EOPD仅使用教师的top-k概率并重新归一化，导致被省略词元的目标概率为零；论文证明，这会持续将学生的top-k总概率推向1，使教师分布本身不再是目标函数的驻点。ReTaCo保留各个top-k词元，并把其余词元聚合为单一残差符号，同时使用期望等于全词表反向KL的单样本估计器。其参数β可连续控制向top-k集合转移的概率质量，并保持集合内相对概率不变；理论分析给出了唯一最优解及其单调性质。实验覆盖三组教师—学生模型，ReTaCo在多数数学与代码基准上优于EOPD。"
purpose = "- 为低通信、低存储成本的在策略蒸馏提供更一致的截断概率监督方案。\n- 揭示top-k重新归一化会造成目标分布偏移，设计蒸馏损失时应显式保留尾部概率质量。\n- 可通过β在忠实匹配教师分布与强化高概率词元之间进行可控权衡。\n- 单样本反向KL估计器有助于在避免完整词表教师分布传输的同时维持无偏训练信号。"
notes = "标题：ReTaCo: Residual-Target Control for On-Policy Distillation\n来源：arXiv\n链接：https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.39275\n摘要：On-policy distillation (OPD) trains a student on its own generated prefixes with token-level teacher feedback, but transmitting or storing the teacher's full-vocabulary distribution at every token is costly. Entropy-aware OPD (EOPD) adds forward supervision to reverse KL to help the student recover plausible tokens it underestimates, using only the teacher's top-$k$ probabilities to limit cost. Because EOPD renormalizes these probabilities, its target assigns no mass to the omitted vocabulary. We prove that the resulting loss keeps pushing the student's top-$k$ mass toward one even after the student matches the teacher's relative probabilities within the top-$k$ set, so the teacher itself is not a stationary point whenever the omitted tokens have positive teacher probability. We propose Re"


[[items]]
id = "trust-the-critic-more-actor-critic"
title = "Trust the Critic More：基于动作分块的 Actor-Critic 训练"
subtopic = "信用分配"

source = "arXiv"
summary = "AC2 利用可信度受控的评论家为长轨迹动作块分配信用，无需将每条轨迹采样至终局，并以更少解码计算量超越 GRPO。"
paper = "https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.39247"
code = ""
dataset = ""
link = ""
content = "论文提出 Actor-Critic with Action Chunking（AC2），将长轨迹切分为约 1 万 token 的动作块，并由学习到的评论家评估动作块结束时的状态。该方法不必等待终局奖励即可更新策略，从而减少完整轨迹生成带来的训练开销。为提高评论家信用分配的可靠性，AC2 引入按题目判断的局部就绪机制，并在可用时向评论家提供此前成功轨迹的参考解答。在 FineProofs-RL 上训练 Qwen3-4B 后，AC2 在 IMO-ProofBench 上以少 2.5 倍的解码 FLOPs 超过 GRPO 的 18.5% 峰值验证分数。"
purpose = "- 为长程推理强化学习提供比统一终局优势更细粒度的信用分配方案。\n- 表明经过可靠性门控的评论家可直接参与策略更新，而不应仅被用作基线估计。\n- 通过缩短采样轨迹和减少达到目标性能所需的训练步数，显著降低解码计算成本。\n- 动作块粒度为评论家评估与逐 token 归因之间提供了更稳定、可扩展的折中。"
notes = "标题：Trust the Critic More\n来源：arXiv\n链接：https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.39247\n摘要：Standard language model RL algorithms credit every token of a long rollout with the same advantage determined by the terminal reward. Actor-critic methods can provide finer-grained credit assignment, but learned critics are generally considered too inaccurate to trust when training LLMs with RL. In recent works, even when a critic is present, it is used only for baseline estimation, so every trajectory must be rolled out to its terminal reward. We introduce Actor-Critic with Action Chunking (AC2) that removes the need to roll every trajectory to completion. AC2 instead assigns credit to action chunks: short continuations of prefixes of past trajectories. A learned critic scores the state reached at the end of each action chunk, allowing the policy to update without observing a terminal rew"

paper = "https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.39229"
code = ""
dataset = ""
link = ""
content = "RAIM采用交叉拟合的堆叠逻辑回归，降低多个低成本评判模型之间相关错误对聚合结果的影响。其可采纳性测试根据成员模型自身的输出，判断聚合是否优于最佳单一成员并接近前沿评判模型。由十个不同模型家族组成的评判面板在八项忠实度基准上保留了Claude Sonnet中位数93%的Cohen's κ，平衡准确率平均仅低2.9个百分点。该方法的推理价格约为前沿模型的六十四分之一，但需要使用50至100条领域内标注记录完成一次性校准；当多个成员能力较强且错误互补时，聚合收益最明显。"
purpose = "- 为大规模、高频率的生成内容忠实度监控提供低成本的前沿模型替代方案。\n- 可采纳性测试能够在部署前判断模型聚合是否值得，避免在单一成员已占明显优势时无效增加计算开销。\n- 研究表明，构建评判模型集成时应优先关注成员之间的错误互补性，而非仅增加模型数量。\n- 适用于需要兼顾检测准确率、开放权重部署与推理成本的幻觉审计系统。"
notes = "标题：RAIM: Robust Aggregation of Inexpensive Models for Hallucination Detection\n来源：arXiv\n链接：https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.39229\n摘要：Automatic evaluation of faithfulness increasingly relies on a large language model acting as a judge, yet the most reliable judges are proprietary frontier models, costly and ill-suited to high-throughput monitoring. We investigate whether a panel of cheap open-weight judges (4--9B) can be aggregated to stand in for a frontier one, what the substitution sacrifices, and when it is worth making. We propose RAIM, an aggregation scheme robust to the members' correlated errors, coupling a cross-fitted stacked logistic regression with an admissibility test that, read from the members' own outputs, identifies when aggregating them improves on their best member and stays within reach of the frontier judge. We instantiate RAIM with ten judges from disjoint families across eight faithfulness benchma"

summary = "FYAN 将人类指导、Lean 证明构造与基于证据的语义审计整合为文档级数学形式化流程，显著提升证明成功率及形式陈述与原始规范的一致性。"
paper = "https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.39228"
code = ""
